Catalog number : M00342
Uniform title: sādhanamālā Vol. 1
Author : bhattacharya benoytosh
Description:
This text is a collection of sādhanas, that is, procedures for the worship of Buddhist Tantric deities especially those of the Anuttarayoga Tantras. A description of the deity, its form and colour, vehicle etc along with its related visualization is supplied in each sādhana together with that deitys Mantra and its applications in worship and yogic practice. They are composed by a large range of Buddhist teachers many of eminence. The earliest is the Siddha Saraha who is dated to around 630 CE. Along with him are a number of other great Buddhist Siddhas, such as Nāgārjuna, Kukkuripa, Indrabhūti, Kṛṣṇapāda, śabara and others who belonged to the 7th and 8th century. The last contributors belong to the 12th century which gives us a lower limit for the date of the collection.
Notes:
Data entered by the staff of Muktabodha under the direction of Mark S. T. Dyczkowski.

Revision 0: February 21, 2015
Publisher : Baroda Oriental Institute
Publication year : 1925
Publication city : Baroda
Publication country : India

 p. 1)

sādhanamālā |

ī

oṃ namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvebhyaḥ |

śrīmattrisamayaṃ vande sarvasampatsukhodayam |
bhavadurgatikhinnānāṃ cintāratnamivādbhutam ||

astyeva sādhanaṃ samyak pūrvācāryairihoditam |
kintu vistarabhīruṇā saṃkṣiptamupadiśyate ||

iha khalu śrītrisamayarāje mahātantrarāje mahātantre [k: reads tantramaṇḍale] 
mantramaṇḍale rajomaṇḍale [rajomaṇḍale dropped in all other ṃṣṣ except ṇ] vā 
vidhivallabdhādhikāro mantrī taduktasamayasamvarasthaḥ pūrvasevāṃ cikīrṣuḥ 
parvatāraṇyādiṣu guhāgṛhārāmalayanādiṣu vā viviktavijaneṣu manorameṣu vasan ādau 
tāvadimāṃ mūlavidyāṃ buddhapratimāyā agrato [k: reads rāntada] maṇḍalakaṃ 
[k: ṇa maṇḍalam] puṣpābhikīrṇaṃ kṛtvā praṇamya bodhicittamutpādya 
sarvabuddhabodhisattvebhya ātmānaṃ niryātya tatpraṇāmālambanajāpamabhyasan [k: 
ṇa lamba ; ḍh: syan in] sahasraṃ japet | tataḥ sarvamantrāṇāṃ lakṣajāpaḥ [k ḍh 
a: jāyam] kṛto bhavati | sarvarakṣādimantrāścāsya siddhā bhavanti | tatreyaṃ vidyā

naivedyamantraḥ [ṅ: nivedya] | namastraiyadhvikānāṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ arara 
parara karara balide baliṃ dadāhi mahābali svāhā | tato 
daśadiglokadhātusthitacitrapūjāṅgānyevaṃ niryātayet | praṇāmāñjaliṃ baddhvā ye 
asamā aparigrahā daśadiglokadhātuṣu [k, ḍh a: dhātu] pūjāṅgaviśeṣāḥ 
sthalajā ratnaparvatakalpavṛkṣādayo jalajāḥ sāmudraratnādayaḥ 
kanakapaṅkajādayaśca ye cānye sarvalokadhātuṣu divyamanuṣyakāḥ 
sarvarūpaśabdagandharasasparśādayastān sarvān buddhabodhisattvebhyo 
niryātayāmītyudāharet | manomayāṃstu pūjāmeghānevaṃ [k, ḍh a: moghā] 
pravartayet | pūrṇamudrāṃ saṅkucitāgrapraṇāmāñjalilakṣaṇāṃ [k, ḍh a: 
saṃkuritā ; k, ḍh a: lirla] baddhvā evaṃ vadet |

p. 8)

vidyāmantrabalena ca sarvabuddhabodhisattvaparṣanmaṇḍaleṣu varodārāḥ 
samantabhadrabodhisattvacaryābhirnirhitā [k, ḍh a: rarddhatā] 
mahāpūjāmeghāḥ prasarantāmiti cintayitvā imāṃ pūjādhiṣṭhānakartrīṃ 
mahāvidyārājñīmaṣṭau vārānuccārayet | namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvānāṃn sarvathā 
udgate sphura [k, ḍh a: sthala] himaṃ gaganakaṃ samantataḥ svāhā |

tataḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvānāṃ vicitraguṇavarṇasaṅgītimeghasamudrā 
dharmasaṅgītimeghasamudrāśca sarvalokadhātuviśodhakāḥ sarvatra pravarttantāmiti 
cintayitvā imāṃ stutisaṅgītimeghābhirnirhārakartrīṃ vidyārājñīmaṣṭau 
vārānuccārayet | namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvānāṃ sarvatra saṃkusumitābhinnārājñini 
[k, ḍh a: nāśi] namo'stu te svāhā | tataḥ sarvalokadhātusamudreṣu sarvatra 
sarvasattvātmabhāvasamāḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvanirmāṇameghāḥ sarvārambhaṇebhyo 
niścārya sarvasattvānāṃ sarvaduḥkhāni prajñamayantu sarvalaukikalokottarasampade 
yāvat samantabhadrakāyādiśuddhi [k, ḍh a: kāyādi] prāpayantviti 
[k, ḍh a: yatu iti] cintayitvā imaṃ [k, ḍh a: imāṃ] 
mahāvidyārātramaṣṭau vārānuccārayet | namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvānāṃ namo'stu te 
mahāvajra sarvasattvahitaṅkara tiṣṭha sarvatra sarveṣāṃ dharmāṇāmadhiṣṭhāya 
[k, ḍh a: sarvadharmā] svāhā | sarvaṃ caitat [k, ḍh a: sarvatra 
tatra] pūjādikaṃ mantrādibalenāvitathaṃ sambhavatīti śraddheyam | kuśalamūlaṃ 
caitat anuttarasamyaksambodhāvevaṃ pariṇāmayet | yathā buddhā bhagavanto 
abhyanujānanti pariṇāmyamānametat

p. 9)

kuśalamūlaṃ tathāhaṃ pariṇāmayāmītyanena puṇyenāhaṃ samantabhadracaryā 
[k, ḍh a: caryā] bodhimabhisambudhya sarvasattvānapi [ṅ: needs sarva 
sarva] samantabhadracaryāśuddhau pratiṣṭhāpayāmīti [ṅ: payeyamiti] |

tataḥ sarvabuddhabodhimattvādhiṣṭhānapratilambhārthaṃ tanmudrāṃ baddhvā pūrvoktaṃ 
śatākṣaramanusmaret pañcākṣaraṃ vā | hastadvayamanyonyaghṛṣṭaṃ

rmadhya] aṅguṣṭhadvayaṃ samamūrdhvamukhaṃ dhārayet śatākṣaramudrā | 
pañcākṣaramudrā tu [k, ḍh a: tu dropped in] sampuṭāñjaliṃ kṛtvā 
tarjanīdvayenāṅguṣṭhāgraṃ [k, ḍh a: tarjanīnā] pīḍayet śeṣāstu tathaiva 
sūcyākārāḥ [k, ḍh a: sūryā] | mantraḥ | namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvānāṃ 
āḥ vīra huṁ khaṁ |

[k, ḍh a: ṣṇīṣa] dharmacakraṃ vā badhnīyāt |

madhyamā] madhyapradeśinyau sūcyākāreṇa [k, ḍh a: drop saṃsakte

[k, ḍh a: nanakhaṃ instead of nakhena nakhaṃ] nakhamālabheta

p. 10)

mantraṃ sakṛduccārya sthito niṣaṇo vā japet | mārādibhirnābhibhūyate 
siddhiścāsyābhimukhībhavati | tataḥ śīghrasiddhyarthaṃ tāvad dharmodayamudrāṃ 
baddhvā tanmantramanusmaret | vāmahastena muṣṭiṃ baddhvā tarjanīkaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhau 
ca prasārayedūrdhvaṃ dharmodayamudrā | mantraḥ | namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvānāṃ 
āḥ sarvathā sarvatrāṇake svāhā | svamudrayā ca mantraṃ [k, ḍh a: mantra] 
sakṛduccārya samayaṃ darśayet | svamudrā tu yathāyogaṃ veditavyā [k, ḍh a: 
vaditavyā] |

tataḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvapraṇāmālambanajāpamabhyasan [ṅ: bhyasyan] 
yathābhilaṣitaṃ mantraṃ na drutaṃ na vilambitaṃ asatsaṅkalpavarjitaṃ 
mantrākṣaragatacittaṃ [k, ḍh a: gataṃ cittaḥ] tāvajjapet yāvanna khedo 
bhavati | tata utthāyārya [k, ḍh a: tyāyāryaṃ] gaṇḍavyūhādisūtrāṇi 
tathāgatahṛdayaṃ sakṛdanusmṛtya vācayet | pūjayitvā antataḥ praṇamya vā 
bhuñjīta | bhuñjatā cānena mantreṇāhāramaṣṭa vārānabhimantritaṃ kṛtvā 
agrapiṇḍaṃ [k, ḍh a: agraṃ] sarvabuddhabodhisattvebhyo nivedya 
madhyamātrayā bhoktavyam | tatrāyaṃ mantraḥ | namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvānāṃ oṃ 
balandade [k, ḍh a: balandada] tejomālini svāhā | bhuktaśeṣād 
vāryācalāyāmoghacaṇḍahṛdayena sakṛdabhimantryotsṛṣṭapiṇḍo dātavyaḥ | sa 
tatrānubandhaḥ [ṅ: tatānubaddha] sukhasiddhitāṃ dadāti | bhuktaviśrāntaśca 
pāpadeśanādikaṃ kṛtvā saddharmārāma eva tiṣṭhet | yadi [k, ḍh a: 
avisakto] śakto bhavati aparāhṇe'pi sarvametat rakṣādiparikaraṃ pūjādikaṃ ca 
kṛtvā japet no cet pūrvāhṇakṛtameva rakṣādi [k, ḍh a: rakṣāvi]

p. 11)

avisarjitaṃ sthitameva [k, ḍh a: sthitanīva] 
dṛḍhamadhimucyāryācalavajramudrayā samantrayā vighnānutsārya 
pañcākṣarādīnāmanyatamena mudrāsahitena rakṣāṃ kṛtvā pūjāstutyādikaṃ vidhāya 
pūrvavat jāpaṃ kuryāt | vikāle ca prākārapañjarādikaṃ visarjya raśmimālinyā kavacaṃ 
kuryāt | anyonyāṅguliśliṣṭāmabhyantaramuṣṭiṃ kṛtvā madhyame sūcyākāreṇa 
prasārayet tarjanīyugalaṃ tasyāstṛtīyaparve [k, ḍh a: bhūmyā] nyaset 
aṅguṣṭhau ca pārśvataḥ | raśmimālinīmudrā | mantraḥ | namastraiyadhvikānāṃ 
tathāgatānāṃ mahāsamayagatiṃ gate samate sama sarvathā sattvatrāṇake [k, ḍh 
a: trāṇaka] dharmadhātvabhyantarasaṅgate svāhā | pūrvarātrāpararātrajāgarikā ca 
saddharmasvādhyāyādinā kartavyā | madhyame yābhe mañcakarahitāyāṃ śayyāyāṃ 
sarvabuddhabodhisattvān sarvāṅgataḥ praṇamanneva svapet [k, ḍh a: praṇamet na 
ca bhayet] vijñaptiṃ ca kuryāt | adhitiṣṭhantu māṃ sarvabuddhabodhisattvā 
anuttarasiddhivaradāyakāśca bhavantu sarvopadravāṃśca praśamayantviti [k, ḍh 
a: pragama] | ayameva ca vidhiḥ pratyahaṃ [k, ḍh a: pratyahaṃ dropped in] 
yāvat paurṇamāsyāṃ lakṣajāpo vā yāvad vā siddhinimittāni prādurbhavanti | tataḥ 
paurṇamāsyāditithiṣu kṛtabhaktacchedopavāsaḥ poṣadhasambarī [k, ḍh a: 
pāsadha] pallavopaviṣṭaḥ kuśaṇḍikopaviṣṭo vā 
caityapaṭapuṭapustakapratimādīnāmanyatamasyāgrataḥ [k, ḍh a: caimyayata] 
kṛtakusumāvakīrṇamaṇḍalakaḥ [k: kṛtamukta ; ṅ: varṇa] 
kṛtarakṣāpūjādiparikaraḥ pūrvavad

p. 12)

dharmodayamudrāṃ baddhvā [ṅ: dayāmbadhvā] tanmantramanusmaret | tataḥ 
svadevatāmudrāṃ baddhvā tanmantraṃ saptāṣṭavārānuccārya [k: saptātavṛ0] 
samayaṃ darśayet | 

tataḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvān praṇamya cakrapūjādikaṃ gṛhītvā 
samantabhadratathāgatakāyādiśuddhimabhilaṣan [k, ḍh a: gatakādi] 
svasamīhitasiddhau hṛdayamādhāya 
sarvabuddhabodhisattvapraṇāmālambanajāpamabhyasan 
sarvauddhabodhisattvapuṇyajñānasambhārānumodanābhyāsacetanayā [k, ḍh a: 
vyetarayā] sandhyārāgāt prabhṛti tāvat japed yāvat arddharātre sūryodaye vā 
taccakrādikaṃ avaśyaṃ prajvalati | jvalite [ṅ: jvalitā] cākāśāt buddhotpāda iva 
mahānimittāni puṣpavṛṣṭidundubhidhvanidivyaghoṣatathāgatasādhukārādīni 
buddhakṣetrakampanādīni cātyadbhutāni [k: cādbhutā] bhavanti 
sarvavidyādharakulāni ca sannipatanti | tairabhiṣicyate | sarvalokadhātuṣu 
buddhabodhisattvārādhakaḥ pañcābhijñaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvābhinandito 
bodhisattvacaryācārī vidyādhararājo bhavati anantavidyādharastrīparivāraḥ [k, ḍh 
a: dharapari] sukhānuyāyī na ca tasmāt kāyāddhīyate | tenaiva ca 
kāyenānupūrvasambhāropacayataḥ [k: yacataḥ] sarvabodhisattvabhūmirākrāmati 
yāvadabhisaṃbuddhyate ceti | anyāśca 
buddhabodhisattvadarśanacintāmaṇibhadraghaṭādisarvalaukikalokottarasiddhayo'nenaiva 
vidhinā [k: vidhāna] sāmānyaviṣayapaṭaladṛṣṭena vā vidhinā tantramavalokya 
nirvicikitsaiḥ sādhanīyāḥ | niyataṃ sidhyantītyayamupāyavatāṃ 

p. 13)

sādhanavidhiḥ anupāyaistu yathāśakti sādhanaṃ kartavyam | yathokte nāhaṃ śakta iti 
nāvasāditavyam [k, ḍh a: nāvasītitavya, ṅ: nāvasātitavyaṃ] | antata 
ekāmapyātmarakṣāṃ kṛtvā sīmābandhanaṃ ca pūjādikaṃ ca cintayitvā 
tanmantrānuccārya yāvadicchaṃ japet sādhayed vā | vīryānurūpaṃ [k: 
nujñāyaṃ] karmānurūpaṃ cāvaśyameva sidhyati | eko'pi trailokyaṃ rakṣituṃ 
kṣamati tantravacanāt | amoghasiddhiścāyaṃ trisamayarājaḥ nirvighnasiddhiśca | 
manīṣitavidhiriti pūrvasevādividhirahitaṃ sarvabuddhabodhisattvālambanamātraṃ kṛtvā 
jagadarthacittena mantrānuṣṭhānam |

atra ca yena tenāpi vidhinā manīṣitena vā mantrāḥ sādhyamānāḥ sidhyanti | tadyathā 
atraivoktaṃ viviktavijane sthāne saṅganikāyaparivarjite [k: citrarvi] 
sattvānanutāpinā vināpi pūjayā [k, ḍh a: pūjā] vināpi paṭena vināpi 
snānādisamudācāreṇa vinipatitenāpi sādhyamavaśyaṃ sidhyatīti | punaruktaṃ dve 
cātyadbhute | yathā yathā mantrāḥ sādhyante tathā tathā anurūpā bhavanti | yena ca 
vidhinā [k: vinā] yojyante [k, ḍh a: yokṣante] tenaiva sidhyantīti | 
punarapyuktam - 

bodhicittaṃ dṛḍhaṃ [k, ḍh a: hṛdayasya] yasya niḥśaṅkā ca 
matirbhavet [k: atibhavet] |
vicikitsā na kartavyā tasyedaṃ sidhyati dhruvam ||

iti | tasmād vīryamutpādya vicikitsāṃ vihāya sādhayitavyamavaśyaṃ sidhyati | atra ca 
siddhyarthinā [k, ḍh a: rthenā] samayarakṣaṇe
 
p. 14)

dṛḍhatarayatnavatā bhāvyam | tanmūlatvāt sarvasiddhīnāṃ ca sa ca samayaḥ | na 
saddharmaḥ pratikṣeptavyāḥ [k, ḍh a: pratijñaptavya] na guruṣvavamanyanā 
kāryā na madyapānaṃ kāryaṃ na mañcaśayyā kāryā na vajrākārā bhañjanīyā na 
laṅghanīyā ityevamādiravaśyaṃ tantre jñātavyaḥ | vistarabhayāttu [ṅ a: 
bhayārttena] na likhyate | snātumicchatā ca 
trisamayajāpināryācalahṛdayenāmoghacaṇḍenaiva vajramudrāyuktena 
sarvamṛttikādisnānīyadravyābhimantraṇaduṣṭotsāraṇasīmābandhādikaṃ [ṅ a: 
mantraduṣṭo] kāryam | tenaiva sarvavighnāḥ praśāmyanti | 

|| iti trisamayarājasya sādhanaṃ samāptam 

kṛtiriyaṃ paṇḍitakumudākaramitipādānām [k: karapādā] ||

p. 15)

2.

namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvebhyaḥ |

asamācalāḥ samatasāradharmiṇaḥ karuṇātmakā jagati duḥkhahāriṇaḥ |
asamantasarvaguṇasiddhidāyino amalācalāḥ samavarāgradharmiṇaḥ ||

gaganasamopamakatā na vidyate [k, ḍh a: naivadyate] 
guṇaleśareṇukaṇike'pyasīmike [k: pyasīmike dropped in] |
sadasattvadhātuvarasiddhidāyiṣu vigatopameṣu asamantasiddhiṣu ||

satatāmalā karuṇavegatotthitā praṇidhānasiddhiravirodhadharmatā [k: 
ranibodha] |
jagato'rthasādhanaparāsamantinīsatataṃ virocati mahākṛpātmanām ||

na nirodhatāṃ karuṇacārikākulā [k: acalā] vrajatetrilokivarasiddhidāyikā 
[ṅ a: vrajāto ; k, ḍh a: lokavara] |
amitāmiteṣu susamāptitāṃ gatā [k, ṅ: gatiṃ] gatiṃ gateṣvapi aho 
[k: a] sudharmatā ||

trisamaye'grasiddhi varadā dadantu [k, ṅ: dadātu] me 
varadānatāgragatitāṃ gatāḥ sadā [k: dānāgra] |

p. 16)

sakalāstrilokivaradāgrasādhakā nāthāstriyadhvagatikā anāvṛtāḥ ||

iti visamayarājakalpoktā vajradharasaṃgītā stutiḥ |

idaṃ tat sarvabuddhānāmadbhutaguṇavistaram [ṅ: sadbhata] |
sidhyanti sarvamantrā vai sakṛduccārite'pi hi ||

anena stotrarājñā vai toṣitāste tathāgatāḥ |
dadanti vipulāṃ [ṅ a vipula] siddhiṃ kalpasthāṃ kalpacoditām ||

darśayanti ca ātmānaṃ āsecanakavigraham |
vairocanamahānāthamakṣobhyaṃ ratnasambhavam ||

amitābhaṃ jinaṃ śuddhamamogharājaṃ ca sarvataḥ |
rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ tattvaṃ pravadanti varāṇi [k: varāṃśi] ca ||

aśeṣāḥ siddhayo ramyā vipulā arthasampadaḥ |
sarvāśāparipūriṃ ca dadanti manasepsitāḥ |
jñānamāyurbalaṃ vegaṃ dadanti paramaṃ śubham [k, ṅ a: guṇaṃ] ||
 
iti | etadeva stotraṃ vajramaṇḍalalaṅkāramahāyogatantre'pi [ṅ: lākāra] 
sānuśaṃsaṃ sampaṭhitamiti |

namo buddhāya |

buddhāṃstraiyadhvikān natvā buddhaputrāṃśca [k: buddhapatraśca] 
bhāvataḥ |
vakṣyāmi samayaṃ kiñcit śrīmattrisamayoditama ||

na saddharmaḥ pratikṣepyo [k, ṅ a: trajvāḥ] na santyajyaḥ [k: 
tantrājñāḥ] kadācana |
sambuddhā bodhisattvāśca na kāryā teṣṭhanādṛtiḥ ||

p. 17)

gurau vidheyā nāvajñā na hantavyāśca dehinaḥ |
na svayaṃ mantramudrāśca kāryā nāśyāśca naiva tāḥ ||

mātsaryaṃ madyapānaṃ ca karaṇīyaṃ na sarvathā |
vajrākārā na laṅghyāśca [k: laṃghāśca] bhañjanīyāśca naiva te ||

na mañcaśayanaṃ kāryaṃ na mudrādiṣvagau [k: diśca] gauravam |
na bāladharmatā kāryā gurau vā devatāsu vā ||

abhicāro na kartavyaḥ suśīle nāparādhayet [ṅ, a: naya] |
nākāryaṃ karaṇīyaṃ vā nānumodyaṃ [ṅ, a: moghaṃ] ca kilviṣam ||

saṃkṣepāt pratikūlaṃ yat tanna kāryaṃ parātmanoḥ |
samayo'yaṃ mahākalpe śrīmattrisamaye smṛtaḥ ||

kaukṛtyamājīvamalaṃ ratiṃ saṃgaṇikāsu ca |
vicikitsakatā bhūriḥ pariṣkāraparigrahaḥ ||

ālasyaṃ līnacittatva ātmotkarṣādayastathā |
antarāyakarā dharmāḥ kalparāje'tra kīrttitāḥ ||

doṣairamībhirnirmuktaḥ pūrvoktasamaye sthitaḥ |
sarvāsaṅgojjhitaḥ śrāddhaḥ sthirasambodhimānasaḥ ||

jagattrayahitodyuktaḥ vāṅmanaḥkāyaceṣṭitaḥ |
siddhiṃ trisamayoktena sādhayed vidhinepsitām [k, ṅ a: dvinemi] ||

trisamayarājasādhanam |

kṛtiriyaṃ paṇḍitaratnākaraguptapādānām ||

p. 18)

3.

namaḥ [ḍh: namo buddhāya, k: namo vajrāsanāya] śākyamunaye 
tathāgatāya |

natvā sarvatathāgatān guṇagaṇādhārān parārthaṃ guroḥ 
śrīvajrāsananāmabhūṣitatanoḥ saṃsāradoṣagrahaiḥ |
aspṛṣṭavarabuddhatādamaśamaṃ tatsādhanaṃ [g: satsādhanaṃ] tattvato 
nānāvarṇatathāgatādiracanādehaṃ tathā [k, ḍh: tadā] likhyate [k: 
kathyate] ||

tataḥ purato bhagavantaṃ vakṣyamāṇavidhinā varṇabhujādisamanvitabuddhaṃ 
sarvatathāgatādisamanvitaṃ [k: sarvatathāgatādisamanvitaṃ found only in] 
vicintya puṣpādikaṃ prayacchet | oṃ namo akṣobhyāya huṁ oṃ vajrapuṣpe huṁ svāha | 
pratyekaṃ vāratrayaṃ dadyāt | oṃ namo vairocanāya oṃ oṃ vajrapuṣpe huṁ [k: huṁ 
dropped in] svāhā | oṃ namo ratnasambhavāya [k: vajrasambhavāya] trāṁ 
oṃ vajrapuṣpe huṁ svāhā | oṃ namo'mitābhāya hrīṁ oṃ vajrapuṣpe huṁ svāhā | oṃ 
namo'moghasiddhaye [g: siddhi] khaṁ oṃ vajrapuṣpe huṁ svāhā | oṃ namo 
locanāyai loṁ [g: laṃ] oṃ vajrapuṣpe huṁ svāhā | oṃ namo māmakyai māṃ oṃ 
vajrapuṣpe huṁ svāhā | oṃ namaḥ pāṇḍarāyai pāṁ oṃ vajrapuṣpe huṁ svāhā | oṃ 
namastārāyai tāṁ oṃ [g: oṃ dropped in] vajrapuṣpe huṁ svāhā | 
punarapyakṣobhyarūpavajrāsanāya prabhūtaprabhūtataraṃ puṣpaṃ dadyādanenaiva 
krameṇa | oṃ vajradhūpe huṁ svāhā oṃ vajragandhe

p. 19)

huṁ svāhā oṃ vajranaivedye huṁ svāhā oṃ vajradīpe huṁ svāhā ityādibhiḥ [k, 
ḍh: ityādi] pūjāvidhiṃ kṛtvā maitryādibhāvanāpuraḥsaraṃ tataḥ 
svahṛdyakārapariṇataṃ [k: svahṛdaya] candramaṇḍalaṃ tasyopari huṁkāraṃ 
suvarṇavarṇaṃ [k: varṇaṃ dropped in; g: varṇa] tadraśminirgatān 
gurubuddhabodhisattvān dṛṣṭvā tataḥ snānapūjāmeghaprasaraiḥ [k, ḍh, g: 
read puṣpādibhiḥ after snāna ; k: mogha, k, b: medhya] sampūjya tataḥ 
pāpadeśanāṃ puṇyānumodanāṃ [k: puṇyānumodanāṃ dropped in] 
pariṇāmanāṃ triśaraṇagamanaṃ jinamārgāśrayaṇaṃ [k, ḍh, g: jina 
dropped in ; g: śrayāt] bodhicittotpādaṃ ca kuryād ratnatrayaṃ me śaraṇamityādinā | 
tataḥ śūnyatāṃ vibhāvayet [k: vilā] | sarvadharmān niḥsvabhāvarūpān 
vibhāvya [k, b: vayitvā] akṣararūpaṃ bodhicittasvarūpaṃ 
prabhāsvaramātmānaṃ paśyet | dṛḍhīkaraṇārthaṃ mantramuccārayet oṃ 
svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśuddho'ham | tadeva jyotīrūpapariṇataṃ 
[k, ḍh: rūpaṃ tatpari] candramaṇḍalam | tasyopari huṁkāraṃ 
akārādiṣoḍaśasvarapariveṣṭitaṃ punarapi kakārādivarṇena [k, ḍh: varṇena 
dropped in] pariveṣṭitaṃ śuklavarṇaṃ sakalasamastapariṇataṃ candramaṇḍalarūpaṃ 
[k, ḍh: rūpaṃ dropped in] vicintayet | tasyopari huṁkārākṣarabījaṃ 
[k, ḍh, g: huṃkāra] tatpariṇataṃ vajraṃ pītavarṇaṃ tatrāpi 
vajravaraṭake [k, ḍh: varaṭakaṃ] huṁkāraṃ samastametat [g: 
samantrame] pariṇamya vajrāsanarūpaṃ 

p. 20)

suvarṇavarṇaṃ [ḍh: vaṇaṃ dropped in] tasya caturmārāsanam [k, b: 
rmmārairāsanaṃ] | te ca skandhakleśamṛtyudevaputramārāḥ [k: skanda ; k, 
b: māraṃ] gauraraktakṛṣṇaśyāmavarṇāḥ | yathākrameṇa mūrtimeṣāṃ vibhāvya 
ebhirmāraiḥ parighaṭitasiṃhāsanaṃ tasyopari [k, b: sanasyopari] 
viśvapadmaṃ vajraṃ ca tatra sthitaṃ bhagavantaṃ dvibhujaṃ vajraparyaṅkaniṣaṇaṃ 
[k, k, b: paryaṅka] savyakaraṃ bhūsparśamudrānvitaṃ 
avasavyamutsaṅgasthitaṃ raktavastrācchāditagātraṃ śāntamātmānaṃ vibhāvayed yogī 
| tato'haṅkāramutpādayet [k, ḍh, g: huṁkāraṃ] | oṃ 
dharmadhātusvabhāvātmako'ham | tato bhagavato dakṣiṇe maitreyaṃ [g: 
maitriyaṃ] bodhisattvaṃ gauravarṇaṃ [k: gauravarṇaṃ dropped in] dvibhujaṃ 
jaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇaṃ savyagṛhītacāmararatnaṃ [k, ḍh: savye] avasavyena 
[k, ḍh, g: savye] nāgakeśarapuṣpacchaṭādhāriṇaṃ tathā vāme 
lokeśvaraṃ  bodhisattvaṃ śuklavarṇaṃ dakṣiṇe gṛhītacāmararatnaṃ vāmena 
kamaladhāriṇaṃ [g: dhāriṇāṃ] bhagavanmukhaṃ vyavalokayantau [k, 
ḍh: kayanto] tau [k, b: tau only in] bhāvayet |

paścāt ṣaḍaṅganyāsaṃ kuryāt | bhagavato cakṣuṣorubhayoścandramaṇḍalaṃ 
[k, ḍh, g: bhagavataścakṣu] tadupari kṣiṁkāraṃ śuklavarṇaṃ 
śrotrayoścandramaṇḍalopari jaṁkāraṃ nīlavarṇaṃ nāsāpuṭayoścandramaṇḍalopari 
khaṁkāraṃ pītavarṇaṃ jihvāyāṃ candramaṇḍalopari gaṁkāraṃ raktavarṇaṃ 
lalāṭacandramaṇḍalopari skaṁkāraṃ śyāmavarṇaṃ stanadvaye [k: 
tantramadhye, ḍh, g: stanamadhye] candramaṇḍalopari saṁkāraṃ 
śuklavarṇaṃ iti ṣaḍaṅganyāsaṃ

p. 21)

vibhāvya tataḥ svahṛdi candramaṇḍale huṁkārapariṇatavajraṃ [k, b: ṇataṃ 
vajra] tadvajravaraṭake candramaṇḍalopari huṁkāraṃ tatsarvaṃ pariṇāmya 
[k, ḍh: ṇamya] jñānasattvaṃ samayasattvavad [g: sattvambi] 
vicintayet |

tataḥ śirasi candramaṇḍalaṃ tasyopari uṁkārapariṇataṃ cakraṃ śuklavarṇaṃ tanmadhye 
candrastha-uṁkāraṃ ceti [k: cintaṃ] bhagavataḥ kāyaviśubhāvanā | kaṇṭhe 
candramaṇḍalaṃ tasyopari [k, ḍh, g: maṇḍalasyopari] 
āḥkārapariṇatāṣṭadalapadmaṃ [k, ḍh: ākāra] raktavarṇaṃ tanmadhye 
[k: tanmadhya] candramaṇḍalasthaṃ āḥkāramiti [k, b: omits āḥkāramiti 
maṇḍalasthaṃ] bhagavato vāgviśuddhibhāvanā | nābherūrdhvaṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ 
tasyopari huṁkārapariṇataṃ vajraṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tanmadhye candramaṇḍalasthaṃ 
[k: maṇḍala] huṁkāramiti bhagavataścittaviśuddhibhāvanā | 
buddhatvaphaladāyinī buddhasiddhiriti | tataḥ ākāśasthitaṃ bhagavantaṃ 
hṛdbījaraśminā [ḍh: hṛdayabīja, g; raśmanā k, b: hṛdbījārciṣā] 
ākṛṣya jaḥ huṁ vaṃ hoḥ ityakṣaracatuṣṭayena purataḥ sthāpayet | tasmai 
arghyapādyācamanaṃ [ḍh: dyāsanaṃ, g: dyācamaṃ] prokṣaṇaṃ 
[k, g: prokṣaṃ] ca dadyāt | paścāt kṣīrādibhojanaṃ prayacchet 
ityamṛtāsvādo [k: tāmbādau] vihitaḥ | tataḥ sarvabuddhāna namasyāmītyanayā 
stutyā [k, ḍh: stutvā dropped in] stutiṃ kuryāt | tato jñānasattvena saha 
samayasattvamekīkṛtya samayastvaṃ samayastvaṃ [ḍh: adds a third 
samayastvaṃ] samayamahaṃ oṃ āḥ huṁ [ḍh: huṁ dropped in] ityanena 
mantreṇādvayaṃ kuryāt | paścād

p. 22)

bhāvanākhinno japaṃ kuryāt | oṃ āḥ vajra huṁ [k, g: huṃ phaṭ] svāhā iti 
japamantraḥ [k: ayaṃ] | paścāt samyaksambodhau puṇyaṃ pariṇāmya visarjya 
[k, b; visarjayitvā] viharet yathecchayeti | anena puṇyena 
sarvasattvāstathāgatajñānaprāptāḥ [k, b: sarve dropped in] śīghraṃ 
bhavanti |

|| vajrāsanabhaṭṭārakasādhanopadeśavidhiḥ [g: sāmadhye] parisamāptaḥ ||

4. 

pūrvoktena [ḍh: pūrvoktavi] vidhānena śūnyatābhāvanānantaraṃ 
candramaṇḍale nīlaṃ huṁkāramakārādisitaṣoḍaśasvarapariveṣṭitaṃ [k: 
ṣoḍaśavara] tataḥ sitakakārādicatustriṃśadvyañjanapariveṣṭitaṃ [k: 
tastriṃśa] sarvametatpariṇamya candramaṇḍalaṃ tadupari punastadeva [k: 
nyasta] bījaṃ tatpariṇāmena pītavajraṃ tadvaraṭake'pi tadbījaṃ sarvametat pariṇamya 
śrīmadvajrāsanabuddhabhaṭṭārakamātmānaṃ jhaṭiti niṣpādayet [k, ḍh:

bhūsparśamudrādakṣiṇakaraṃ [g: bhūspṛśa]

vicintya oṃ dharmadhātusvabhāvātmako'hamiti advayāhaṅkāraṃ

p. 23)

kuryāt | tadanu bhagavato dakṣiṇe maitreyaṃ [g: maitriyaṃ] bodhisattvaṃ 
suvarṇagauraṃ dvibhujaṃ jaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇaṃ gṛhītacāmaradakṣiṇakaraṃ 
nāgakeśarapallavadharavāmakaram [g: pallavāmara ; k: cāmarakaraṃ] | tathā 
vāme lokeśvaraṃ bodhisattvaṃ śuklaṃ jaṭāmukuṭinaṃ [k: jaṭamukuṭiṃ] 
cāmaradhāridakṣiṇabhujaṃ kamaladhārivāmakaram | etaddvayaṃ

kurvīta] | bhagavataścakṣuṣoścandrasthasitakṣiṁkāraṃ [k: ścandrasthitaṃ] 
śrotrayoścandrasthanīlajaṁkāraṃ tato [ḍh: tato puṭe; k; omits tato] nāsāpuṭe

[k: candra āḥ] vāgviśuddhibhāvanā | śirasi candre sitaoṃkārajāṣṭāracakre

candrasthasavajrahuṁkāramiti cittaviśuddhibhāvaneti | tato

|| iti vajrāsanasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

5.

namo vajrāsanāya |

ādau mukhādiśuddhiṃ vidhāya svahṛdyakārapariṇatacandre pītahuṁkāraṃ paśyet | 
tatkiraṇairākṛṣya gurubuddhādīn pūjayet | tato'pi pāpadeśanādikaṃ vidhāya

[k, ḍh: codanā] rūpakāyamabhinirmāpayet sattvārthavidhaye | 
śaśima.ḍalamadhye pītahuṁkāraṃ paśyet akārādisitaṣoḍaśasvaraveṣṭitam [ḍh: 
sita dropped in] tato'pi bahiḥ sitakakārādivarṇasamūhairveṣṭitaṃ [k: sahasre]

cchatra] | tathā vāmato lokeśvaraṃ śuklaṃ dakṣiṇakareṇa cāmaradharaṃ

śaśimaṇḍalāvasthitaṃ jaṁkāraṃ nīlaṃ nāsāpuṭe 

p. 25)

candrasthapītakhaṁkāraṃ jihvāyāṃ candrasthaṃ raktagaṁkāraṃ [g:

|| iti vajrāsanasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

6.

in] |

b: and kh: svahṛdi purato is dropped in] 
candrasthasitahrīḥkāravinirgataraśmibhirgurubuddhabodhisattvān purato buddhādīn

triśaraṇagamanādikaṃ [k: śaraṇādikaṃ] kuryād ratnatrayaṃ me [k: 
trayaśaraṇa] śaraṇamityādinā | yāvantaḥ sattvāḥ sattvasaṃgrahena saṃgṛhītāḥ 
aṇḍajā vā jarāyujā vā saṃsvedajā [k: svasva] vā aupapādukā vā rūpiṇo vā

dropped in] sarve mayā anupadhiśeṣanirvāṇadhātau [k: anupaviśeṣeṇa]

[k, b: tadanu] svahṛdaye [k, kh, ḍh, g: hṛdaye]

tat] | tat 

p. 27)

punastatraiva praveśayet [k: praveśastat] | tatpapariṇatamātmānaṃ

oṃ maṇipadme huṁ iti [k, ḍh, g: iti dropped in] jāpamantraḥ | tata

dṛḍho me bhava sutoṣyo me bhava

supoṣyo me bhava anurakto me bhava sarvasiddhiṃ me prayaccha sarvakarmasu ca me cittaṃ

7. 

padmakulodbhavaṃ nāthaṃ sarvajñakṛtamaulinam |
praṇamya sādhanaṃ [g: sālaṃ] vakṣye sarvarogavināśanam ||

prathamaṃ tāvadācāryānugatā [k: cāryān gatā] siddhiḥ - 

tasmācca [ṭhe metre in both these ślokas is faulty] sarvabhāvena guruṃ 
pūjayed yatnataḥ |
guruṇā parituṣṭena [k: parituṣṭo na] karmasiddhiḥ prajāyate ||

maṇḍalapraviṣṭasya siddhiranujñātā ca sarvathā |
svasamayasambaraṃ rakṣayan sidhyate dhruvam ||

mukhaśaucādipūrvakaṃ devagṛhe paṭādigatabhaṭṭārakamavatārya svahṛdaye 
ādyakṣareṇa [k: ādyādya] candramaṇḍalaṃ tasyopari aṣṭamasya caturthakaṃ 
bījaṃ saptamadvitīyenāsanaṃ [k, g: dvitīya nā] prathamacaturthena maṇḍitaṃ

p. 29)

ratnatrayaṃ me śaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ pratidiśāmyagham [k: moghaṃ] |
anumode [k: modya] jagatpuṇyaṃ buddhabodhau dadhe manaḥ ||

ābodheḥ śaraṇaṃn yāmi buddhaṃ dharmaṃ gaṇottamam |
bodhau cittaṃ karomyeṣa svaparārthaprasiddhaye ||

utpādayāmi [īn a instead of these two lines we have utpādaṃ yāmī aṅga | īn the

nimantrayāmi bahusarvasattvān |
iṣṭāṃ cariṣye varabodhicārikāṃ buddho bhaveyaṃ jagato hitāya ||

iti | praṇidhipūrvakaṃ sarvadharmanairātmyaṃ bhāvayet anena mantreṇa | oṃ 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'ham | 

bījaṃ māyopamākāraṃ [ḍh: adds vicintya after māyopamākāraṃ] 
tridhātukamaśeṣataḥ |
dṛśyate spṛśyate caiva yathā māyā hi [k: māyā ti] sarvataḥ |
na copalabhyate caiva sarvasya jagataḥ sthitiḥ ||

ityadhimucya | tato'nādikālīnamasatkalpanābījamapanīya svabhāvamadhimuñcet [k: 
mucet] | oṃ svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśuddho'ham | tataḥ 
pūrvoktabījaniṣpannaṃ padmaṃ tasyopari hrīḥkāraṃ tatsarvaṃ [g: tasava] 
niṣpanne sati śrīmallokanāthaṃ [k: dhīman] vajrapadmagarbhacandrasthaṃ 
vajraparyaṅkaṃ śaśiprabhaṃ kundenduvarṇamujjvalaṃ jaṭāmakuṭadharaṃ śāntaṃ 
amitābhakṛtaśekharaṃ [k: kṛśeṣvaraṃ] vyāghracarmanivasanaṃ 
caturbhujaṃ nānālaṅkārabhūṣitaṃ dakṣiṇekaṁre 

padmamaṇivibhūṣitaṃ dvau hastau saṃyuktau sarvarājendramudrā hṛdi saṃsthitaṃ 
tato'haṅkāraṃ kuryāt ahameva lokeśvara iti | tato jñānasattvamākṛṣya

8.

pūrvavidhānena candramaṇḍalaṃ saptatṛtīyakaṃ bījaṃ trayodaśākrāntaṃ

9. 

hrīḥkārajñānaniṣpanno hālāhalo [k: halāhalo] mahadvapuḥ [g: 
mahāvapuḥ] |
trinetraṃ trimukhaṃ caiva jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitam ||

arddhacandradharaṃ devaṃ kapālakṛtaśekharam |
jinabimbajaṭāntaḥsthaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam ||

sitāravindanirbhāsaṃ śṛṅgārādirasānvitam |
ṣaḍbhujaṃ hasitaṃ [k: sahitaṃ] vaktraṃ vyāghracarmasuvāsasam [g: 
carmāśasavāsanaṃ] ||

p. 32)

dakṣiṇe kare varadaṃ dvitīye cākṣamālikam [k: mālikāṃ] |
tṛtīye śaranarttanaṃ vāme padmavibhūṣitam ||

dvitīye kucadharaṃ caiva tṛtīye dhanureva ca |
dakṣiṇe triśūlaṃ caiva [k: lañcai, k: jantu] sarpeṇa pariveṣṭitam ||

vāme pūrṇakapālaṃ tu puṣpairnānāsugandhibhiḥ |
raktapadmasthitaṃ caiva lalitākṣepasaṃsthitam [k: tākṣaya] ||

ratnācalaguhāntaḥsthaṃ bhāvayed yogacintakaḥ |
sphuradbuddhamayairbimbaiḥ [k: mayotbambai] vividhaiḥ prātihāryakaiḥ ||

sarvāhāraṃ tu bhuñjāno nirvikalpasamādhinā |
antarjalpo japenmantrān bhāvayet kramayogataḥ |
kṣipraṃ ca prāpyate bodhiranuttarasukhāvahā [k: sukhāmahā] ||

|| sarvakāmaprasādhanahālāhalaparitoṣaṇavajrā [k: parito vana] nāma samādhiḥ ||

p. 33)

10.

hṛccandre nyasya [k: jīḥkāraṃ] hrīḥkāraṃ dharmanairātmyabhāvanam |

nabhaḥsamān ||

taṃ sitaṃ raktavarṇaṃ tu padmarāgasamadyutim [g: mayaṃ] ||

pañcabuddhamakuṭadharaṃ [k, ḍh: mukuṭaṃ] harṣeṇotphullalocanam 
[ḍh: harṣotphullalocanaṃ | k: harṣotphullavanaṃ] |

mayūropari madhyasthe niṣaṇaṃ candramaṇḍale ||

sasṛksāra] |
caityāntaḥsthamahākarma kūṭāgāravihāriṇam |
bhāvayed vajradharmāgryaṃ [k: dharmagraḥ] nityaṃ bodhimavāpnuyāt ||

bhāvanākhinno hṛccandre nyasya mālāmivāvikṣiptacitto japet | tatrāyaṃ japyo mantraḥ 
oṃ vajradharma hrīḥ |

|| sādhanopāyikā saṃkṣiptā vajradharmasya ||

p. 24)

p. 35)

[k: sphuraṇaskṛtaiḥ] |

tatrāyaṃ devatākāropadeśaḥ -

maṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā rakṣāṃ kuryāt | oṃ maṇidhari vajriṇi rakṣa rakṣa māṃ huṃ phaṭ 
svāhā | oṃ vajrarekhe [k: rekha] huṁ maṇḍalamantraḥ | oṃ āgaccha bhagavan 
maṇḍalakasiṃhāsane oṃ āḥ iti mantrakṛto'dhyeṣaṇāyāṃ [k: dhyeyanā] 
kṛtamaṇḍalamadhyasiṃhāsanopari sahasradalapadmasthaṃ pañcātmakaṃ pūjayediti | 
tatra pūjāmantrāḥ - oṃ vajrapuṣpe huṁ | oṃ vajradhūpe huṁ | oṃ vajragandhe huṁ | 
oṃ vajrāloke [k, g: vajragandhe] huṁ | oṃ vajrāhāre huṁ | iti saṃpūjya 
saṃstutya mantrajāpaṃ kuryāt | oṃ sarvatathāgatapūjāmeghaprasarasamūhe huṁ | 
pūjādhiṣṭhānamantraḥ

p. 45)

oṃ [k: oṃ āṁ trāṁ tṛṁ huṁ phaṭ svāhā] āḥ tāṁ suṁ bhṛṁ haṁ phaṭ 
phaṭ svāhā | oṃ sarvatathāgatasulalitanamitairnamāmi bhagavantaṃ jaḥ huṁ vaṁ hoḥ 
pratīccha kusumāñjaliṃ [k: māñjalinmatha] nātha hoḥ iti vandanāmantraḥ | oṃ 
muḥ svāhā iti visarjanamantraḥ | oṃ kha kha khāhi khāhi [k: khāhihi] gṛhṇa 
gṛhṇa gṛhṇantu sārvabhautikā imaṃ baliṃ svāhā iti balimantraḥ | atha 
pūjārambhakāle evamuccārya cittaṃ śodhanīyaṃ yat sarvaduścaritebhyo viratiṃ karomi 
[k: ṅkaroṣi] sarvabuddhabodhisattvaśikṣāṃ śikṣiṣye [k: śiṣye] yāvat 
pūjāvidhiṃ na samarthayāmi [k: samandhayāmi, ḍh: samandhayāmi] | 
yadyevaṃ na kriyate tadā rāgajaṃ dveṣajaṃ mohajaṃ kuśalamūlaṃ syāt | iha sarva eva 
sattvā [k: sarvatvā] rāgadveṣamohāśayāḥ | tathāhi rāgajakuśalamūlena [k: 
rāgaku] nandopanandau nāgarājānau dveṣajena rāvaṇaḥ [k: rāgeṇa g: 
ramaṇāḥ] mohajena vaiśravaṇo yakṣo bhūtaḥ | ityato rāgadveṣamohādīn parihṛtya 
cittaviśuddhyā [k: cittaśuddhyā] karuṇāmayacittena sarvāṇyeva 
dānādikuśalamūlāni kartavyāni |

|| iti khasarpaṇasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

p. 46)

16.

namaḥ [ṭhis sādhana is only to be found in k, b. see fol. 28] khasarpaṇāya |

prathamaṃ tāvanmantrī sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ svahṛdi akāreṇa candramaṇḍalaṃ tataḥ 
hrīḥkāraṃ sitavarṇaṃ tadudbhūtasitaraśmibbhiragrato'mbare gurubuddhabodhisattvān 
paśyet | tāna svahṛdbījaraśmibhiḥ sampūjya pāpadeśanādikaṃ kuryāt | oṃ 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'hamiti adhiṣṭhayet | ātmānaṃ śūnyatāyāṃ 
vyavalokya pūrvoktahrīḥkāreṇa sphuraṇasaṃharaṇakrameṇa lokeśvararūpamātmānaṃ 
bhāvayet sarvāṅgasitavarṇaṃ jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍitam amitābhakṛtaśekharaṃ 
sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaṃ arddhaparyaṅkinaṃ dakṣiṇe varadahastaṃ vāme padmadharaṃ 
padmacandrāsanastham | dakṣiṇapārśve āryavasudhārāsudhanau vāmapārśve 
bhṛkuṭīhayagrīvī | bhagavataḥ śirasi candre oṃ kaṇṭhe āḥ hṛdi huṁ evaṃ vibhāvayet 
| dhyānāt khinno yogī mantraṃ japet | tatrāyaṃ mantraḥ oṃ hrīḥ svāhā |

|| āryāvalokiteśvarakhasarpaṇasādhanaṃ samāptamiti ||

p. 47)

17.

namaḥ siṃhanādāya |

dvibhujaikamukhaṃ śuklaṃ trinetraṃ siṃhavāhanam |
siṃhanādamahaṃ vande sarvavyādhiharaṃ gurum ||

ādau tāvanmantrī mukhaśucādikaṃ kṛtvā mano'nukūle sthāne sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ 
śukla-akārapariṇataṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ tadupari śuklahīḥkāraṃ [k: hrī] 
hṛdi paśyet | tadraśmibhistraidhātukamavabhāsyākaniṣṭhabhuvanavarttinaṃ 
siṃhanādaṃ sarvagurubuddhabodhisattvānākṛṣya purata ākāśadeśe saṃsthāpya tadanu 
pūjāpāpadeśanādikaṃ kṛtvā caturbrahmavihārān vibhāvya 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvāḥ [k: svabhāvātmako] sarvadharmāḥ oṃ 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'haṃ ityanena mantreṇa śūnyatāmadhitiṣṭhet | tataḥ 
praṇidhānamanusmṛtya [ḍh: manusmṛtya, g: matrasmṛtya] 
śuklapaṁkārapariṇataṃ kamalaṃ [k: omits kamalaṃ pariṇataṃ] tadupari 
śukla-akārapariṇataṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ tadupari śukla-āḥkārapariṇataṃ 
[k: ā] śvetasiṃhaṃ tadupari śukla-aṁkārapariṇataṃ [ḍh: 
oṃkāra] śvetapadmaṃ tadvaraṭake śuklahrīḥkāraṃ sphuradraśmivisaraṃ etet 
sarvaṃ pariṇamya siṃhanādarūpamātmānaṃ paśyet sarvāṅgaśuklaṃ dvibhujaṃ 
ekamukhaṃ trinetraṃ jaṭāmukuṭadharaṃ amitābhālaṅkṛtaśirasaṃ [k: 
śiraḥ] mahārājalīlayā sthitaṃ siṃhāsane vyāghracarmāmbaradharaṃ 
sphuratpañcatathāgataṃ aṃsalulitapañcacīraṃ arddhacandrālaṅkṛtaṃ [ḍh: 
candrāṅkitaṃ] vāmahastasthitaśuklapadmopari sitakhaḍgaṃ tatsamīpasthitaṃ 
śuklapadmopari nānāsugandhikusumaparipūrṇaśuklakaroṭakaṃ 

p. 48)

dakṣiṇe sitapadmopari sitaphaṇiveṣṭitaṃ sitatriśūladaṇḍaṃ evaṃbhūtaṃ bhagavantaṃ 
dhyātvā dhyānāt khinno mantrī mantraṃ japet | tatrāyaṃ mantraḥ oṃ āḥ siṃhanāda huṁ 
phaṭ svāhā | vidhiratra pratimākṛteḥ [k: pratimākṣato] paṭagatasya vā 
bhagavataḥ purataḥ pratimaṇḍalamekavāraṃ dhāraṇyā | tatreyaṃ dhāraṇī - namo 
ratnatrayāya nama āryavalokiteśvarāya bodhhisattvāya mahāsattvāya mahākāruṇikāya 
tadyathā oṃ akaṭe vikaṭe nikaṭe [k, g: reads trikaṭe after nikaṭe] kaṭaṃkaṭe 
karoṭavīrye svāhā | apatitagomayamabhimantrya aṣṭau maṇḍalakān kuryāt | 
pratimaṇḍalaṃ trayodaśa vārān āvartayan dhāraṇīṃ pratimaṇḍalāmalitaśeṣagomayaṃ 
dhāraṇyā [k: ndhāraṇī, ḍh: dhāraṇīṃ g: ndhāraṇā] sapta 
vārānabhimantrya tena vyādhiṃ [k: vyāpiṃ] pralepayet | saptame divase trayodaśe 
divase ekaviṃśatitame [k: viṃśatiñcānta] vā pañcānantaryakāriṇo'pi sidhyanti 
[k, g: sidhyati] yadi na sidhyanti [k, g: sidhyati] tadā'haṃ 
pañcānantaryakārī syām |

|| siṃhanādasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

vidhāya sādhanaṃ dhanyaṃ yadalābhi [k: yadacalābhiḥ] śubhaṃ mayā |
siṃhanādasya nāthasya nirvyādhi syāt tato jagat ||

|| kṛtiriyaṃ paṇḍitāvadhūtaśrīmadadvayavajrapādānām ||

p. 49)

18.

namo lokanāthāya ||

pūrvavat kramayogena lokanāthaṃ śaśiprabham |
hrīḥkārākṣarasaṃbhūtaṃ [k: hrīḥkārakṣasaṃ] jaṭāmukuṭamaṇḍitam ||

vajradharmajaṭāntaḥsthaṃ aśeṣaroganāśanam [k, g: 
aśeṣārāganāgaraṃ] |
varadaṃ dakṣiṇe haste vāme padmadharaṃ tathā ||

lalitākṣepasaṃsthaṃ tu mahāsaumyaṃ prabhāsvaram |
varadotpalakarā [k: valadālaṅkārā, kh: varadotpalā |] saumyā tārā 
dakṣiṇataḥ sthitā ||

vandanādaṇḍahastastu hayagrīvo'tha [g: yovo'rtha] vāmataḥ |
raktavarṇomahāraudro [g: raktarṇo] vyāghracarmāmbarapriyaḥ ||

tadvaraṭakāṣṭadale [ṭhe passages from tadvaraṭakāvṛdale 
maṇḍalasyānusārataḥ cannot be found in any other recension except k ; ḍ, dh: 
tadvākyāvṛdale] padme maitreyādiṃ ca vinyaset |
maitreyaḥ pītavarṇaśca nāgapuṣpavarapradaḥ |
kṣitigarbhaḥ śyāmavarṇaḥ kāśaṃ cābhayaṃ tathā ||

vajrapāṇiśca śuklābho vajrahasto varapradaḥ |
khagarbho nabhaḥśyāmābho [ḍ, dh: naśyāmābho] 
cintāmaṇivarapradaḥ ||

mañjughoṣaḥ kanakābhaḥ khaḍgapustakadhārakaḥriṇaḥ |
gaganagañjo [ḍ, dh: gaganagajā] raktavarṇo nīlotpalavarapradaḥ ||

p. 50)

viṣkambhī tu kṣāravarṇo ratnottamavarapradaḥ |
samantabhadraḥ pītābho ratnotpalavarapradaḥ ||

dhūpādicaturdevī ca vajrāṅkuśyādidvāragāḥ [ḍ, dh: vajrīkuśādi] |
varṇāyudhe yathāpūrvaṃ maṇḍalasyānusārataḥ ||

evaṃvidhaiḥ samāyuktaṃ lokanāthaṃ prabhāvayet |
sarvakleśamalātīto bhavet pūrṇamanorathaḥ ||

atra mantraḥ oṃ hrīḥ svāhā |

|| iti lokanāthasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

19.

loṁbījāt [k, g: lāṃ] sarvaṃ pūrvavad vidhāya śuddhiparyantaṃ tato loṁ 
[k, g: lāṃ] pariṇamya lokanāthaṃ candraprabhaṃ sarvābharaṇojjvalaṃ 
jaṭākumuṭinaṃ padmacandropari paryaṅkinaṃ vāme padmadharaṃ dakṣiṇe varadaṃ 
cintayet | samayamudrā | muṣṭidvayaṃ baddhvā madhyāṅgulyau padmasaṃkocākāreṇa 
[k: sakācākā] yojayet | oṃ maṇipadme huṁ mantraṃ japet |

|| lokeśvarasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

p. 51)

20.

namaḥ siṃhanādāya 

prathamaṃ mukhaśaucādikaṃ kṛtvā sukhāsanastho yogī svahṛdi sūryamaṇḍale 
āḥkāraṃ dṛṣṭvā purato gurubuddhādīnānīya pāpadeśanādikaṃ kuryāt | tataḥ 
śūnyatāmāmukhīkṛtyādhiṣṭhāya ca praṇidhimanusmaret | tataḥ akārapariṇataṃ 
[k: ākāra ; k: has huṁkāraṃ tatpariṇataṃ after this] candraṃ tasyopari 
oṃkārapariṇataṃ raktapadmaṃ tadupari āḥkārapariṇataṃ śvetasiṃhaṃ tasyopari candre 
hrīḥkārasambhavaṃ siṃhanādabhaṭṭārakaṃ śvetaṃ jaṭāmakuṭinaṃ trinetraṃ 
dvibhujaṃ tapasviveśadharaṃ mahārājalīlayā sthitaṃ vāmahastādutthitapadmopari 
jvaladūrdhvakhaḍgaṃ dakṣiṇe sitatriśūlaṃ sitaphaṇiveṣṭitaṃ vāme 
nānāsugandhipuṣpaiḥ pūrṇaṃ śvetakapālaṃ amitābhamukuṭinaṃ 
sphuratpañcatathāgataṃ [k, g: spharayet] mahānirmāṇarūpiṇaṃ dhyāyāt | 
japamantraḥ oṃ āḥ hrīḥ siṃhanāda huṁ phaṭ |

|| iti siṃhanādasādhanam ||

p. 52)

21.

namo ratnatrayāya |

nama āryāvalokiteśvarāya bodhisattvāya mahāsattvāya mahākāruṇikāya tadyathā oṃ 
akaṭe vikaṭe nikaṭe kaṭaṃkaṭe karoṭe karoṭavīrye svāhā | eṣā bhagavata 
āryāvalokiteśvarasya purataḥ pratyūṣe apatitagomayenāṣṭau ma.ḍalakān kṛtvā 
pratimaṇḍalaṃ trayodaśa vārānuccārayitavyā | tataḥ sapta vārān 
gomayaśeṣamabhimantrya vyādhimupalepayet sarvavyādhīnupaśamayati | yadi saptame 
divase trayodaśe vā ekaviṃśatitame vā [k, ḍh: vā omitted] divase 
pañcānantaryakāriṇo'pi na sidhyanti [k: sidhyati] tadā ahaṃ [k, ḍh: 
ahaṃ dropped in] pañcānantaryakārī syāmiti | 

|| siṃhanādānāma dhāraṇī samāptā ||

p. 53)

22.

namaḥ siṃhanādāya [k, b: this namaskāra is only to be found in] |

prathamaṃ tāvanmantrī mukhaśaucādikaṃ kṛtvā mṛduviṣṭare [k, ḍh, g: 
mṛduvivṛre upaviśya not tobe found in] upaviśya sukhāsanasthaḥ svahṛdi 
candramaṇḍale sitahrīḥkāraṃ dṛṣṭvā tadraśmisamākṛṣṭān purato 
gurubuddhabodhisattvān dhyāyāt | tadanu pūjāpāpadeśanādikaṃ kṛtvā śūnyatāṃ 
vibhāvya oṃ [īnstead of the mantra oṃ śūnyatā etc. k, b: has svabhāvaśuddhāḥ 
sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśuddho'haṃ] śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'haṃ 
ityanenādhitiṭhet | tato [k, b: paścāt] jhaṭiti paṁkārapariṇataṃ raktapadmaṃ 
taduparisiṁkārapariṇataṃ [k: tadupariṇataṃ] śvetasiṃhaṃ tasyopari candre 
hrīḥkārasambhavaṃ siṃhanādalokeśvararūpamātmānaṃ dhyāyāt 
śuklamamitābhajaṭāmukuṭinaṃ trinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ tapasviveśadharaṃ mahārājalīlayā 
sthitaṃ vāmahastādutthitapadmopari [k: dutthitapari] jvalatkhaḍgaṃ dakṣiṇe 
sitatriśūlaṃ sitaphaṇiveṣṭitaṃ vāme nānāsugandhipuṣpaiḥ pūrṇaṃ śvetakapālaṃ 
sphuratpañcatathāgataṃ [k, b: tathāgatāḥ] mahānirmāṇarūpiṇaṃ 
[k, b: rūpaṃ g: rūpitāṃ] dhyāyāt iti | japamantraḥ oṃ āḥ hrīḥ siṃhanāda 
huṁ phaṭ [k, b: adds svāhā after phaṭ] |

|| simhanādasādhanaṃ samāptam [this colophon is not to be found in any other ḍ, 
dh: except k, b] ||

p. 54)

23.

tadanantaraṃ dhāraṇī bhavati | namo ratnatrayāya nama āryavalokiteśvarāya bodhisattvāya 
mahāsattvāya mahākāruṇikāya tadyathā oṃ akaṭe vikaṭe nikaṭe kaṭaṃkaṭe karoṭe 
karoṭavīrye svāhā | ayaṃ mantropacāraḥ [g: atropa] bhagavato'grataḥ pratyūṣe 
apatitagomayenāṣṭau maṇḍalakān kṛtvā pratimaṇḍalake [k, b: maṇḍale] 
trayodaśa vārānuccārayet | gomayaśeṣaṃ saptābhimantrya vyādhimupalepayet [k: 
vyādhimūlepayayet] sarvavyādhīnupaśamayati | yadi saptame divase trayodaśe divase 
ekaviṃśatitame [k, g: viṃśatime] vā divase pañcānantaryakāriṇo'pi na 
sidhyatyayaṃ tadā'hameva pañcānantaryakārī bhaviṣyāmi |

|| siṃhanādadhāraṇī samāptā ||

24. 

namaḥ khasarpaṇāya |

natvā khasarpaṇaṃ nāthaṃ nāthabhūtaṃ ca duḥkhinām |
saṃkṣiptāt vistaraṃ kiñcit sādhanaṃ tasya likhyate ||

prathamaṃ tāvanmantrī pāṇipādādikaṃ vāriṇā prakṣālya paramaśucirbhūtvā vijanagiri 
guhāyāṃ sthāne śucau vā kvacit cittānukūle mṛduviṣṭarāmalopaviṣṭaḥ svahṛdi 
prathamasvarapariṇataṃ nirdoṣadoṣākaramaṇḍalamālokya cetasā tanmadhye 

p. 55)

śaradindugataṃ [g: śaradinduśataṃ] kiraṇāvalīprojjvalaṃ hrīḥkārabījaṃ 
[k, g: hrīḥkāraṃ] paśyet | tato'pi hrīḥkārabījāt niḥsṛtya 
jaganmohatamastomadhvaṃsakāribhiraṅkuśākārairmarīcivisarairdaśasu [k, g: 
jananmo ; k, g: mbhoma ; k, g: yansa] dikṣu ye cānantalokadhātavaḥ 
avatiṣṭhante tān sarvān prakāśya tatra sthitā apyasaṃkhyeyā aprameyāśca 
gurubuddhabodhisattvā nabhodeśe cākṛṣya saṃsthāpyante | paścāt teṣāṃ 
nabhodeśavartināṃ [g: vṛtti] paramakāruṇikānāṃ gurubuddhabodhisattvānāṃ 
puṣpadhūpadīpagandhamālyavilepanacūrṇacīvaracchatradhvajaghaṇṭāpatākādi##-
omits yatkiñcit puṇyānumodanāṃ kuryāt] asyāṃ jātāvanyāsu vā 
jātiṣvanādinidhane jātisaṃsāre saṃsaratā mayā pāpakaṃ karma kāyena vācā manasāpi 
kṛtaṃ kāritaṃ kriyamāṇamanumoditaṃ tatsarvaṃ bhagavatāṃ [k: sujātiṣṭharādi ; 
g: bhagavato] trailokyamahotsavānāṃ gurubuddhabodhisattvānāṃ purataḥ 
pratideśayāmītyanena vidhinā pradeśya [k: pratideśya] punarakaraṇasaṃvaraṃ 
[k: puṇaraṇa] pratigṛhya puṇyānumodanāṃ kuryāt | 
saṃbuddhapratyekaśrāvakabuddhānāṃ [k: śrākānāṃ] tatsutānāmapi 
bodhisattvānāṃ sattvānāmapi trailokyodaravarttināṃ yadeva kuśalaṃ tatsarvaṃ 
anumodayāmīti | tadanu ratnatrayaśaraṇagamanam | buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchāmi 
dvipadānāmagryam dharmaśaraṇaṃ gacchāmi virāgāṇāmagryam saṅghaśaraṇaṃ 

p. 56)

gacchāmi gaṇānāmagryamiti [k: gaṇanā] | paścānmārgāśrayaṇam [k: 
yaścātmārgātrayaṇāṃ] | mārgaḥ sambuddhoktaḥ sa cāśrayaṇīyo mayā nānya iti | 
tadanantaramadhyeṣaṇāṃ [k: madhyerpaṇāṃ] kuryāt | sattvārthamāsaṃsāraṃ 
kurvantu bhagavantaḥ [k, ḍh, k: bhagavantaḥ omitted in ] 
saṃbuddhāstatsutā api mahābodhisattvāstiṣṭhantu māmā [k: māpari, ḍh, k: 
mahāpari, g: māṃ pari] parinirvāṇāditi [k: nirvānniti, g: 
nirvāntviti] | tadanu yācanāṃ tathāvidhāṃ niruttaradharmadeśanāṃ 
bhagavantastathāgatā [k: stathāgata] deśayantu [k: deśāyaṃ] yayā 
tvaritameva sattvāḥ saṃsārāgādhasāgaraṃ tarantīti | tadanu yācanānantaraṃ 
puṇyapariṇāmanāṃ kuryāt | saptavidhānuttarapūjayā yadeva kuśalamutpannaṃ tadeva 
saṃbodhaye [k: saṃbodhiṃ] pariṇāmayāmīti | athavā samāsataḥ 
saptavidhānuttarapūjāsūcakān ślokānamūn paṭhet - 

kāyena vācā manasā kṛtaṃ yat pāpaṃ jinānāṃ purato diśāmi |
lokatrayāṇāṃ kuśalaṃ tu sarvaṃ nityaṃ pramodādanumodayāmi ||

ratnatrayaṃ yāmi sadāhamatra mārgaṃ jinoktaṃ śaraṇaṃ śraye [k: 
śreye, g; śaye] ca |
sattvārthamatrāpi kurvantu buddhāḥ sutāśca teṣāṃ satataṃ bhavasya [g: 
vasya] ||

p. 57)

tiṣṭhantu yāvat sthitireva tāvat nirvāntu māṃ te pitaraḥ prajānām |
saṃdeśanāṃ te nanu tādṛśīṃ vā dharmasya kurvantu mahāprabhāvāḥ ||

saṃsārasindhoḥ sahasāpi sattvāḥ pāraṃ yathā yānti sukhaṃ prabhūtam |
puṇyaṃ prabhūtaṃ yadihāpi sarvaṃ saṃbodhaye tat pariṇāmayāmi ||

jinebhyastatsutebhyaśca gurubhyo jagatāmaham |
ātmānaṃ sarvabhāvena niryātayāmi sarvadā ||

ityamībhiḥ saptavidhāṃ pūjāṃ vidhāya oṃ āḥ huṁ muriti visarjayet | tadanantaraṃ 
pramāṇāni catvāri maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣāṇi [k, g: yakṣāmi] 
vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa [ḍh, b: lakṣaṇamāna] dhyāyāt | tatreyaṃ maitrī 
sarvasattveṣu atiśayitāhitaikaputrakasnehalakṣaṇā [k: sattvairatiśayita ; k, 
ḍh, b: putrasneha] | karuṇā tu kīdṛśī ? agādhāpārasaṃsārasāgaramadhye 
[k, g: saṃsārasārasāgara] patitānantasattvadhātūn 
samuddharāmītyadhyāśayaḥ | muditā punaḥ kīdṛśī ? dhātutrayāvasthitānāṃ 
sattvānāṃ yāni [k: pānimucitāni] sucaritāni teṣu tadbhogaiśvaryādiṣu [k: 
omits tadbhogaiśvaryādiṣu] hṛṣṭacittatā [k: hṛṣṭadittayā] 
keyamupekṣā ? pratighānunayanibandhanamapahāya [k, ḍh, b: 
pratighātanamani] hitāhiteṣu sattveṣu paramahitācaraṇaṃ [k: hitāvaraṇāṃ] 
catuḥpramāṇabhāvanānantaraṃ sarvadharmaprakṛtipariśuddhatāṃ [k: omits tāṃ 
ahamapi] 

p. 58)

cintayet | sarva evāmī dharmāḥ prakṛtyā svarūpeṇa pariśuddhāḥ ahamapi 
prakṛtipariśuddha ityādikamāmukhayet | imāmeva sarvadharmaprakṛtipariśuddhatāṃ 
oṃ svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśuddho'hamityanena dṛḍhīkuryāt | na ca 
prakṛtipariśuddhe [k, ḍh, b: pariśuddhina] sarvadharme [k, 
ḍh, b: sarvadharma] sadyo muktiprasaṅgo durvāraḥ syāt | satyaṃ paramayaṃ 
prasaṅgo'saṅgata eva | kuto'saṅgataḥ ? saṃsārahetukarāgādimalaśavalatvāt 
sarvadharmāṇām | kathaṃ punaḥ tadapagamaḥ [k: tadupaśamaḥ] syāt ? 
viśiṣṭamārgabhāvanayā | tayā sa niruddhyate | ata evāha bhagavān nirodhaḥ sākṣāt 
kartavya iti | tasmāt prakṛtipariśuddhāḥ sarvadharmā ityādi vacanamupapannameva 
ityalaṃ bahubhāṣitayā | sarvadharmaprakṛtipariśuddhatāmāmukhīkṛtya 
sarvadharmaśūnyatāṃ dhyāyāt | tatreyaṃ śūnyatā manomātramevedaṃ tena tenākāreṇa 
prakāśātmakaṃ [k: prakāśātmaṃ] pratibhāsate yathā svapne nāsti manaso 
bāhyaṃn [k, ḍh, b: vākyaṃ] manogrāhyaṃ [k: śrāvyaṃ] 
grāhyābhāvāt [k: śrāvyā] | grāhakamapi mano nāsti | tataśca manaḥsvarūpāḥ 
[k: kharūpāḥ] sarvadharmāḥ | teṣāṃ 
grāhyagrāhakādisakalakalpanāprapañcaśūnyatātattvaṃ [k: śrāṇa ; k: 
saṃkalpa] paramārtha iti yāvat | ayamartha advaitaprakāśamātrātmakaṃ sacarācaraṃ 
jagaditi cintanīyam | imāmeva śūnyatāṃ oṃ śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'haṃ 
ityamunā mantreṇādhitiṣṭhet | tadanu kalaṅkāṅkanirmukta-

p. 59)

kṣapākaramaṇḍalāruḍhaṃ [k: rudyat ḍh, b: rūḍhā] śubhrātiśubhraṃ 
[k: śubhātiśubhyat, ḍh, b: śubhrātiśubhrā] hrīḥkārabījaṃ 
bhagavantaṃ śrīmatkhasarpaṇalokeśvaraṃ 
śaradamalahariṇāṅkamaṇḍalaśatodayātiśubhradehaṃ [k: śaradasahari] 
paramaśṛṅgārojjvalaramaṇīyamūrtiṃ nānāratnādyābharaṇavirājitavigrahaṃ [k: 
virājivigrahaṃ] jaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇaṃ [k: drops ṇaṃ ruhopari] 
vikasitapuṇḍarīkalocanaṃ bhagavadamitābhajinaratnaviśobhitaśirodeśaṃ 
hemavālukāsalilakṣālitāvadātāmbarāvṛtaśarīraṃ [g: hima ; ḍh, b: 
vālukāśanikṣāli ; ḍh, b: dātāṃ vurā] 
śakaṭacakrapramāṇamatikamanīyasitāmbhoruhoparisthitaṃ [ḍh, b: 
śatacakra] niṣkalaṅkavidhumaṇḍalāsanasthaṃ arddhaparyaṅkinaṃ savyakareṇa 
varadaṃ avasavyena [k: varadaṃ avasavyena dropped in] 
vikasitamanohārisitāravindadharaṃ evambhūtaṃ bhagavantaṃ niṣpādya 
tadrūpamātmānaṃ vibhāvayet | bhagavato dakṣiṇapārśve cāryatārā sudhanau 
vāmapārśve ca bhṛkuṭī hayagrīvau cintanīyāviti | śirasi niṣkalaṅkendumaṇḍalopari 
oṃkāram tadanu kaṇṭhe vimalasomamaṇḍalopari āḥkāram hṛdaye 
tvamalakumudabandhumaṇḍalopari huṁkāraṃ paśyet | tadanena krameṇa yāvadicchati 
tāvad bhāvayet | tadanantaramasyaiva samayasattvarūpasya bhagavato lokanātharūpasya 
[k: vācasya] 
hṛdisthakalaṅkāṅkaviviktaśaśimaṇḍaloparisthitakārtikendukundasannibhahrīḥkāra##
-##bījavinirgatāparyantaraśmivisarairstrailokyājñānāndhakārā- [g: reads 
kamalaṃ after hṛdistha ; g: omits ndhakārāpahāribhiḥ]

p. 60)

pahāribhirgatvā [g: omits gatvā jñāna] anādisaṃsiddho bhagavān 
jñānasattvarūpaḥ sudūrād dvīpadeśādānīyate [k, ḍh, b: sudūrāddhipaṃ 
deśā ; k: dānīya] | samānīya tameva [k: samānīya omitted in] bhagavantaṃ 
[k: bhavantaṃ] purato nabhaḥpradeśe ca saṃsthāpya 
nānāratnakhacitacāmīkarādibhājanāvasthitasugandhodakena surabhikusumena ca 
tasyaivānītajñānasattvarūpasya bhagavato [k: sattvasya] lokanāthasya 
caraṇārghaṃ dattvā tadanu 
divyapuṣpadhūpadīpanaivedyagandhamālyavilepanacūrṇacīvaracchatradhvajaghaṇṭā##-
in] bhagavantaṃ jñānasattvātmakamarcayet | punaḥ punarabhyarcya stutvā ca jaḥ huṁ 
vaṁ hoḥ ityanenākṣaracatuṣṭayena mudrāṃ darśayet | kiñciducchritaṃ [k: 
ducchanaṃ, ḍh, a: ducchṛtaṃ, g: ducchubhaṃ] saṃpuṭāñjaliṃ 
kṛtvā madhyame sūcīkuryāt śeṣāścāṅgulyaḥ kiñcit saṅkocya 
sammukhamasaṃśliṣṭā dhārayet aṅguṣṭhau tarjanīdvayasamīpe sthāpayediti 
vikasitamakalamudreyam | anayā mudrayā tamevānītaṃ bhagavantaṃ 
jñānasattvalakṣaṇaṃ paritoṣyāpi samayasattvarūpe bhagavatyantarbhāvya oṃ āḥ [k: 
jhāḥ] huṁ ityanenānayoradvaitamadhimuñcet | tadanantaraṃ 
kalaṅkapaṭalanirmuktaśubhrāṃśumaṇḍalamadhyāvalīnāṃ [k: śutāṃśu] 
anauṣadhīśamaṇḍalaśatakaranikarojjvalahrīḥkārabījavinirgatā asaṃkhyeyā aprameyāśca 
bhagavataḥ śrīkhasarpaṇalokeśvarabhaṭṭārakadaśadigvartino'nekalokadhātūn 
[k, ḍh, b: kādaśe] avabhāsya tatra sthitānāmapi

p. 61)

sattvānāṃ svarṇamāṇikyamuktārājapaṭapravālavaiḍūryendranīlādiratnavarṣaṇena 
[k, g: rājapada] dāridryādiduḥkhamapaharanti | 
niruttarakṣaṇikanairātmyādidharmadeśanāyāḥ pīyūṣadhārāprabandhena tān 
santarpayanti | punaḥ punaranavaratanānāprakāraṃ [k: punacaraṇata, ḍh, a: 
bharaṇavata] parārthaṃ kṛtvā jagadapi bhagavallokeśvararūpeṇa niṣpādya tatrāpi 
himārcimaṇḍalamadhyavartinī [k: mātri] 
sphaṭikamaṇikiraṇasaṅkāśahrīḥkārabīje ta eva bhagavantaḥ 
samāgatyāntarbhavantītyevamādisphuraṇasaṃharaṇakrameṇa yāvat khedo na jāyate tāvad 
bhāvayet | tatrāpi bhāvanākhinno [k: bhāsaṇāt] mantraṃ japet | tatrāyaṃ 
mantraḥ oṃ hrīḥ svāhā | mantro'yaṃ bhagavato lokanāthasya hṛdayam | 
asādhāraṇaprabhāva evāsau mantrarājaḥ sarvaireva jinavarairabhyarcitaḥ [ḍh, b: 
vabhyarcya] satkṛto namaskṛtaśceti | samādherutthito jagallokeśvararūpaṃ vīkṣya 
tadahaṅkāreṇa yatheṣṭaṃ viharet iti | prāyo'nena vidhinā bhagavantaṃ cintayet 
khaḍgāñjanādipramukhasakalā [k, g: prakha] mahāsiddhayaḥ samāgatya 
pādayoḥ patanti kiṃ punaḥ kṣudrasiddhayaḥ ? api tu tāśca nitarāmeva | ye 
vijanavanaśmaśānagiriguhāsīnā [k, ḍh, b: vara] bhagavantaṃ 
bhāvayanti te ca acirādeva niyatamatra bhagavantaṃ paśyanti | svayameva 
bhagavāṃsteṣāṃ āśvāsapraśvāsādikaṃ [k: āśvāsādikaṃ] dadyāt | yātu 
[k, ḍh, b: yāvat] kiṃ bahu-

p. 62)

vacanīyaṃ [k: kinvacanīyaṃ] paramātidurlabhaṃ buddhatvaṃ [k: buddhatvaṃ 
dropped in] api teṣāṃ pāṇitalāvalīnavadarakaphalamivāvatiṣṭhati [k, 
ḍh, b: miva tiṣṭhati] iti |

kiñcid vistaraṃ śrīkhasarpaṇalokeśvarasādhanaṃ samāptam |

yat puṇyamarjitaṃ tena yātu lokaḥ puraṃ muneḥ ||

|| kṛtiḥ sthavirānupamarakṣitānām ||

25.

siṃhanādamahaṃ vande sarvavyādhiharaṃ gurum |
bhāvanāyogamātreṇa mucyate sarvakilviṣāt [ḍh: kilviṣaiḥ] ||

prathamaṃ tāvat mukhaśaucādikaṃ kṛtvā śucivastraprāvṛtaḥ pavitrabhūmau 
sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ svahṛdi candre hrīḥkāraṃ vibhāvya tena raśminākṛṣya 
sarvatathāgatān pūjayitvā pāpadeśanādikaṃ kuryāt | tato maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣāśca 
[k: caturvibhāśca] vibhāvya svabhāvaśuddhamantroccāraṇapūrvakaṃ 
śūnyatāṃ bhāvayet oṃ svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśuddho'haṃ oṃ 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'haṃ iti | etadanantaraṃ [k: tadana] 
pratibhāsamātrakaṃ svakāyamavalokyaṃ raktarephapariṇataṃ [g: ripha] 
raktāṣṭadalapadmopari

p. 63)

hrīḥkārapariṇāmena śvetasiṃhaṃ tasyopari pṛṣṭhacandre hrīḥkāraṃ saraśmikaṃ 
tenaivākṛṣya sarvatathāgatapraveśenātmānaṃ siṃhanādaṃ lokeśvararūpaṃ bhāvayet 
śvetavarṇaṃ trinetraṃ jaṭāmukuṭinaṃ nirbhūṣaṇaṃ [g: vibhūṣaṇaṃ] 
vyāghracarmaprāvṛtaṃ siṃhāsanasthaṃ mahārājalīlaṃ candrāsanaṃn [k: 
candrāsanaṃ dropped in] candraprabhaṃ bhāvayet | dakṣiṇe sitaphaṇiveṣṭitaṃ 
triśūlaṃ vāme nānāsugandhikusumaparipūritapadmabhājanaṃ 
vāmahastādutthapadmopari [ḍh: dupasthitaṃ] jvalatkhaḍgaṃ svakāye 
pañcatathāgataṃ sphurantaṃ paśyet | tato hṛdbījenākṛṣya jñānasattvaṃ 
praveśayitvā tathāgatān sphāryābhiṣiñcedātmānaṃ [k: sthāpyā] 
maulāvamitābhamudraṇaṃ cintayet | tato mantraṃ japet devatāmūrtinā | tatrāyaṃ mantraḥ 
oṃ āḥ hrīḥ siṃhanāda huṁ phaṭ svāhā | tadanu sugandhādimaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā 
pūjārthaṃ puṣpādikaṃ ḍhaukayitvā [k: ḍhauṣayitvā] arcayet | 
punarmālāmantraṃ japet | oṃ namo ratnatrayāya nama āryāvalokiteśvarāya bodhisattvāya 
mahāsattvāya mahākāruṇikāya | tadyathā oṃ akaṭe vikaṭe nikaṭe [k: adds kaṭake and 
ḍh trikaṭe after vikaṭe] kaṭaṅkaṭe karoṭe karoṭavīrye svāhā | anena mantreṇa 
maṇḍalamṛttikāṃ gṛhītvā ekaviṃśativārān āvartya [k: ārttya] vyādhiṃ 
pralepya svastho bhavati |

iti [ḍh: omits iti and samāptaṃ int he colophon] siṃhanādalokeśvarasādhanaṃ 
samāptam ||

p. 64)

26.

prathamaṃ tāvat mantrī sukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ svahṛdi 
paṁkārapariṇatāṣṭadalakamalasyopari akārapariṇāmena [k: omits akārapari] 
candramaṇḍalaṃ tasyopari hrīḥkāraṃ saraśmikaṃ dṛṣṭvā tena 
raśminākṛṣyākaniṣṭhabhuvanavarttilokeśvaraṃ purato nabhaḥsthalaṃ dṛṣṭvā 
pāpadeśanādikaṃ kuryāt | tadyathā pāpadeśanā puṇyānumodanā puṇyapariṇāmanā 
triśaraṇagamanaṃ ātmabhāvaniryātanā adhyeṣaṇā bodhicittotpādanaṃ kṛtvā 
caturbrahmavihāravān bhāvayet - maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣāḥ | tataḥ śūnyatāṃ 
bhāvayet oṃ śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'ham | pratibhāsamātraṃ [k: adds 
tena raśminā after pratibhāsamātraṃ] svakāyaṃ [k: khakāryaṃ] vyavalokya 
hṛdi madhye paṁkārajāṣṭadalapadmasyopari [k: paṃ rajā] akāreṇa [k: 
ākāra] candramaṇḍalaṃ tasyopari 
raktahrīḥkāraraśminākṛṣṭasarvatathāgatapraveśenātmānaṃ lokeśvararūpaṃ 
vibhāvayet | śuklavarṇaṃ jaṭāmukuṭinaṃ dakṣiṇahastavaradaṃ 
taditaraśubhrapadmahastaṃ [g: yamahastaṃ] arddhaparyaṅkinaṃ 
sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitaṃ amitābhamakuṭinaṃ dvātriṃśallakṣaṇadharaṃ 
aśītyanuvyañjanaṃ candrāsanaṃ candraprabhamātmānaṃ vibhāvayet | tato dakṣiṇe 
āryatārāsudhanau vāme bhṛkuṭīhayagrīvau pūrve vairocanaḥ dakṣiṇe ratnasambhavaḥ 
paścime amitābhaḥ uttare amoghasiddhiḥ āgneyyāṃ locanā naiṛtyāṃ māmakī

p. 65)

vāyavyāṃ pāṇḍarā aiśānyāṃ vajradhātvīśvarī [k, g: dhāte] | tato 
hṛdbījaraśminākṛṣya jñānasattvaṃ praveśayet | cakṣurādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ 
kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhānaṃ kṛtvā abhiṣekaṃ prārthayet | 

yathāhi [k: yathābhi] jātamātreṇa snāpitāḥ sarvatathāgatāḥ |
tathāhaṃ snāpayiṣyāmi śuddhaṃ [k: adds tu after śuddhaṃ] divyena 
vāriṇā ||

oṃ [k: has āḥ huṁ after oṃ] sarvatathāgatābhiṣekasamayaśriye [k: 
māśraye] huṁ phaṭ svāhā | locanādidaśadevatāṃ saṃsphārya [k: 
saṃsthārya] pūjāstutiṃ kṛtvā mantramājapet | tatrāyaṃ mantraḥ oṃ hrīḥ svāhā |

|| khasarpaṇasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

27.

namo hālāhalāya |

pūrvavachūnyatāparyantaṃ bhāvayitvā - 

hrīḥkārabījaniṣpannaṃ hālāhalaṃ mahākṛpam |
trinetraṃ trimukhaṃ caiva jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitam ||

prathamāsyaṃ sitaṃ nīladakṣiṇaṃ vāmalohitam |
śaśāṅkārddhadharaṃ [k: śaśakārddha] mūrdhni 
kapālakṛtaśekharam ||

jaṭāntaḥsthajinaṃ samyak sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
sitāravindanirbhāsaṃ śṛṅgārarasasundaram ||

p. 66)

ṣaḍbhujaṃ smeravaktraṃ ca vyāghracarmāmbarapriyam ||

varadaṃ dakṣiṇe pāṇau dvitīye cākṣamālikam [ḍh: mālinaṃ] |
tṛtīye śaranartanaṃ ca vāme cāpadharaṃ [k: kṣaparantathā, g: 
kṣeparantathā |] tathā ||

dvitīye sitapadmaṃ ca tṛtīye stanameva ca ||

vāmajānunā sitāṃ svābhadevīṃ [k: nāmitābhadevīṃ] dadhānaṃ | vāmena 
kamaladharāṃ dakṣiṇena bhujena bhagavadāliṅganaparāṃ kusumaśobhitajaṭākalāpāṃ | 
dakṣiṇapārśve sarpaveṣṭitaṃ triśūlaṃ [kh: begins, fol. 43] vāmapārśve 
padmasthakapālaṃ nānāsugandhikusumaiḥ sampūrṇaṃ raktapadmacandre līlākṣepasthitaṃ 
[k: līlāntopasthitaṃ] vibhāvayet bhagavantam | tato mantraṃ japet oṃ vajradharma 
hrīḥ |

|| iti hālāhalalokeśvarasādhanam ||

28.

namo [this sādhana is only to be found in k, b] hālāhalāya |

siddhaṃ hālāhalaṃ natvā jagadvyādhivināśanam |
mahāmāyāmayaṃ tasya vakṣyāmi sādhanakramam ||

tatrādau tāvanmantrī kvacinmano'nukūle pṛthivīpradeśe sthitvā ādyanutpannāḥ 
sarvadharmāḥ prakṛtiparinirvṛtā māyāsvapnagandharvanagaropamā ityadhimokṣeṇa 
ādhyātmikamevamahaṃjñānamadhiṣṭhāya

p. 67)

daśadiganantaparyantalokadhātuvyavasthitān 
buddhabodhisattvavidyākrodhagaṇānavalambya oṃ sarvavit pūra pūra āvartta āvartta 
herityanena vividhapūjāmedhān sphārayitvā manasābhyarcyābhāvena praṇamya 
dharaṇītalavinyastajānumaṇḍalo hṛdi kṛtāñjalirevaṃ brūyāt samanvāharantu māṃ 
buddhā bhagavantā bodhisattvā mahākṛpāvidyādevyā mahākrodhāḥ 
sarvalokadhātuvyavasthitāḥ - 

ābodhiśaraṇaṃ yāmi buddhaṃ dharmaṃ gaṇottamam |
dadāmi tebhya ātmānaṃ pratigṛhṇantu nāyakāḥ ||

anādimati saṃsāre saṃsaratā mayāsmin vā janmani yatkāyavākyamanobhiḥ pāpakaṃ 
karma ratnatrayācāryāpādhyāyamātāpitābhikṣubhikṣuṇī-sāmānyasattvānāṃ 
sāvadyābhyākhyāṃ saṃvarttanīyam dāridryaṃ saṃvarttanīyaṃ vyādhiṃ saṃvarttanīyaṃ 
| athavā yat svataḥ karma kṛtaṃ kāritamanumoditaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ yuṣmākaṃ purastāt 
yadā sadyaḥ kṛtaṃ yacca tat sarvaṃ animittayogenānupalambhamānaḥ pratideśayāmi | 
anantabhūyo'pyevaṃ kariṣyāmi | yacca prathamacittotpādamupādāya yāvadanuttarā 
samyaksambodhiryāvacca nirupādhiśeṣo nirvāṇadhāturatrāntare 
sarvabuddhabodhisattvavidyākrodhagaṇaśrāvakapratyekabuddhānāṃ anyeṣāṃ ca 
pṛthagjanānāṃ kuśalamūlaṃ tatsarvamanumodayāmi | buddhapremastāvadanumodanayā 
- 

utpādayāmi paramaṃ bodhicittamanuttaram |
yathā traiyadhvikā nāthāḥ sambodhau kṛtaniścayāḥ ||

sarvaṃ caitat kuśalamūlaṃ pariṇāmayāmi | samyaksambodhau samyaksambuddhavarttitayā 
pariṇāmanayā tato vajrāñjalirvibhāvya

p. 68)

oṃ sarvatathāgatasaṃśitāḥ sattvānāṃ sarvasiddhayaḥ saṃpadyantāṃ sarvatathāgatāśca 
māmadhitiṣṭhetām | oṃ svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśuddho'hamiti 
udīrayan svaśirasi muñcet | ata eva svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmā vimokṣāt 
vighnopaśamo bhavati iti nātra sandehaḥ kāryaḥ | proktavikalpaprabandhād vighnān |

tadanu svahṛccandre akārodbhavahṛdayaṃ sphuradamitamayūkhaṃ vinyasya 
sarvadharmanairātmyaṃ bhāvayet | tebhyaḥ svahṛdayākṣarebhyo raśmimeghāna 
saṃsphārya tat sampaśyet sthiracalān sarvabhāvān | hālāhalarūpaṃ niṣpādya vicintayet 
tatraivaṃ praveśya māyopamaṃ sakalaṃ jagadityavagacchan ātmānaṃ hālāhalarūpaṃ 
- 

śaśāṅkakoṭisaṃsṛṣṭaṃ jaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇam |
sitāravindanirbhāsaṃ nīlakaṇṭhaṃ trinetrakam ||

mahāpannagasamuddhṛtaraktapadmopari sthitam |
ratnācalaguhāntaḥsthaṃ vyāghracarmāmbarasuvāsasam ||

paryaṅkena samādhisthaṃ sthitaṃ caturbhujavirājitam |
kuṇḍikākṣadharaṃ vīraṃ amitābhakṛtaśekharam ||

triśūlamūrdhvakhaṭvāṅgaṃ kulikābaddhayaṣṭikam |
cintayet tasya vāmena dakṣiṇena kapālakam ||

kusumaparipūrṇaṃ kulikaṃ ca kṛtaphaṇaṃ bhagavantamavalokayantam |

tataḥ pañcāṅgavinyāsaṃ mudrābhirmantrasaṃhatābhiḥ kuryāt | uttānabhājo 
karayornibandhapārśve cañcalatayā śikhāyāḥ parvadvitīyaṃ khalu saṃspṛśet | 
nyasyeta tālau karapārśvataśca mūlamudreyam |

parītataḥ |
veṣṭayitvā ca tālābhyāṃ avaṣṭabhya śiro bhavet ||

imāmeva nyaset mūrdhni viparītaprayogataḥ |
śikhā bhavati tasyaiva hālāhalaniṣevinaḥ ||

pṛthag muṣṭidvayaṃ baddhvā madhyame kṛtaśṛṅkhale |
tāre śūcī ca tālau ca pārśvataḥ kavaco bhavet |
vikṣipya tālatāmeva kalpayedastrakarmibhiḥ ||

tārā tarjanī tālo'ṅguṣṭhaḥ dhārā'nāmikā madhyā śikhīti paryāyaḥ | athāsāṃ 
mudrāṇāṃ mantrā bhavanti | oṃ saḥ svāhā hālāhalahṛdayaḥ | oṃ jvalini svāhā śiraḥ 
| oṃ prajvālanadīpāya svāhā śikhā | oṃ aghryāya svāhā kavacaḥ sarvāṅge | 
anantaśaktigarbhā phaṭ astraṃ karadvaye | tadeva svayamevāhaṃ āryahālāhala 
ityahaṅkāravānātmānaṃ hālāhalaṃ bhāvayed yāvat khedo na jāyate | sati ca khede 
svahṛccandre svajvālahuṁkāraṃ vinyasya tanmayūkhāvabhāsini ca paryanteṣu mantreṣu 
- 

mantrākṣarāṇi vinyasya kuryāt japaṃ samāhitaḥ |
hṛdayaṃ vā japenmantraṃ mālāmantramathāpi vā ||

hṛnmantrakoṭijapte tu mālāyā lakṣajāpayā |
sarvathā tasya sidhyanti sampattivyādhayo'khilāḥ ||

yāvadicchaṃ japaṃ kṛtvā bhāvanāmapi śaktitaḥ |
kṣamayitvā tato nāthaṃ preṣayet mūlamudrayā ||

atha paṭavidhānena bhagavantamārādhayitumicchati tadā paṭe pūrvoktarūpaṃ lakṣayet 
ityayaṃ paṭavidhiḥ |

p. 70)

dvitīyaṃ kathyate - 

suvarṇavarṇaṃ yadi vā trinetraṃ bhujadvayopetasunīlakaṇṭham |
ratnācalasthaṃ susamāhitaṃ tanuṃ halāhalaṃ piṅgalajaṭaṃ likhecca ||

ākrāntapādau pucchau vasunīlarūpāvarddhaṃgatoddaṇḍakṛtasvakāyau |
pārśvadvaye nāthamudīkṣamāṇau likhecca sarpau purataḥ kapālam ||

puṣpairvicitraiḥ paripūrṇametat padadvayaṃ hastadvayaṃ samantāt |
pramāṇametat kathitaṃ jinena adhastu jānusthitadhūpahasto niveśanīyaḥ khalu 
sādhako'tra ||

ato'nyatamapaṭaṃ prasārya prātarevāpatitagomayena maṇḍalakaṃ kṛtvā mūlamudrayā 
mantrasahitayā āvāhayedabhinayena paṭākṛtau pañcāṅganyāsaṃ ca kuryāt 
pūrvoktavidhibhireva mudrābhiḥ samantrasahitābhiḥ | tato mūlamudrayaiva 
pañcopacāreṇa mānasībhiśca pūjābhiḥ sampūjya bhagavantaṃ sambodhayan 
mudrāmupadarśayet kanyaśṛṅkhalelā vā sandhitārāpṛṣṭhe niveśetāṃ | 
śikhādhārāvidhiḥ yau tarjanyau kṛtasūcakau | oṃ visphuliṅgāṭṭahāsakeśari iti 
mantraḥ | tataḥ pūrvoktarūpamātmānaṃ vicintayet | tadrūparahito vā navasṛtaṃ pūrvavat 
mālāmantraṃ trayodaśa vārānuccārayet | maṇḍalakagomayena vyādhiṃ pralepayet | 
ṣaṇmāsaiḥ sarvavyādhayaḥ 

p. 71)

praśāmyanti | athavā lakṣamekaṃ japet | sarvāśāparipūriṇo'tra na kāryā vicāraṇā | 
tatrāryamāryahālāhalahṛdayamantraḥ | namaḥ sarvabuddhabodhisattvebhyo 
mahāpuruṣavṛṣabhebhyo namaḥ āryāvalokiteśvara maheśvara mahāsthāmaprāpta 
āryamaitreya samantabhadra āryavajrapāṇiprabhṛtibhyo mahābodhisattvaprabhṛtibhyo 
namaskṛtvā bhagavannāryāvalokiteśvara tava - 

hṛdayamāvartayiṣyāmi sarvakāmaprasādhanam |
adhṛṣyaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo bhavamārgavināśanam ||

tadyathā | he bodhisattva priyabodhisattva mahābodhisattva he hale āryāvalokiteśvara 
paramamaitracitta hitacitta mahākāruṇika kuru kuru mahāvidyeti dhuru dhuru dharādhara 
calendrācala namaste'stu hrīḥ sarvasattvābhayaprada harihariharivāhanodbhava bhavābhava 
bhavānagha kuru kuru kuru kuru ghuru ghuru suru suru muru muru curu curu 
suprasāditāmalavimalamūrti āryāvalokiteśvara mahākāruṇika kṛṣṇājinadhara 
akṣadhara daṇḍadhara jaṭāṁmakuṭāvalambitapralambadhara mala mala kala kala cala cala 
tala tala nala nala phala phala hulu hulu hulula halāhala ho bodhisattva mahābodhisattva 
namaste'stu hṛṁ hṛṁ hṛṁ kuru kuru hṛdayamanusmara samayamanusmara 
jātimanusmara idaṃ me kāryaṃ kṛtamanorathaṃ me paripūraya svāhā | preṣaṇaṃ ca 
sarvavaditi | 

iti puṇyajanaḥ sarvo bhūyād hālāhala iti |
ahaṃ ca mañjughoṣaḥ syāṃ jagadāśāprapūrakaḥ ||

ābodhi mañjughoṣasya pādāmbujarajorasā |
prajñāpālitanāmnāyaṃ kathitaḥ padmabhṛdvidhiḥ ||

|| āryahālāhalasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

p. 72)

29.

natvāhālāhalaṃ bhaktyā sarvāśāparipūrakaṃ |
saṃkṣepāducyate tasya sādhanaṃ karuṇātmanaḥ ||

ādau manohare sthāne sulipte [k: sulipta] candanādinā |
avatārya paṭādisthaṃ bhagavantaṃ pūjayediti ||

puṣpādiḍhaukanamantraḥ [k: ḍhoṣaṇa] oṃ vajrapuṣpe huṁ oṃṁ vajradhūpe 
huṁ oṃ vajradīpe huṁ ityādinā [k, ḍh, g: ityādi] | oṃ [k, ḍh: 
omits oṃ] sarvatathāgatapūjāmeghaprasarasamūhe sphura [k: stharahimaṃ, 
k, b: ghurahimaṃ] himaṃ gaganakaṃ huṁ sarvapūjāḍhaukanamantraḥ ||

prapūjya puratastasya niṣpadya ca sukhāsane |
maitrīṃ vibhāvya jagati karuṇāṃ ca mahākṛpaḥ ||

vinyasya hṛdaye mantraṃ hrīḥkāraṃ [k: hrīkalaṃ] candrapṛṣṭhataḥ 
[k, g: pṛṣṭhakaḥ] |
paśyedgaganamāpūrṇaṃ [kh: paśyedbhagavanmā] sambuddhaiḥ sannataiḥ 
[k, ḍh, g: sambaddhaistatsutaiḥ] sphuṭam ||

hrīḥkārāṃśujapūjābhistān [hrīḥkārīśukra] prapūjya gatabhramaḥ 
[g: matabhramaḥ, k, b: śatabhramaḥ] |
deśanādyaṃ tu pāpādervidadhyāt puṇyavṛddhaye ||

triratnaśaraṇādyagryaṃ mantramenamudīrayet [k: mantreṇa mudī] |
sarvadharmāgranairātmyadyotakaṃ [k: dharmāgranaināsya] karuṇāmanaḥ 
[k: karuṇāmalaḥ, kh, g: karuṇāvalaḥ] ||

oṃ svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sarvadharmāḥ svabhāvaśuddho'hamiti | 

p. 73)

mantrārthamāmukhīkurvan sarvaṃ vastu [k: sarvasvastra] mahātmanā |
grāhyagrāhakanirmuktaṃ [k: śrāvyaśrāvakani, k, b: 
grāhyagrāhakavini] bhāvayet jñānamātrakam ||

pratyātmavedyaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā hrīḥkāraṃ śaśisaṃsthitam |
tajjamabjaṃ sitaṃ dhyāyāt [k, b: dhyāyāḥ] hrīḥkārānvitapuṣkaram ||

tataḥ saṃsphārya buddhaughaṃ jagad buddhaṃ vidhāya ca |
punaḥ praveśya tatraiva bhūyād hālāhalaḥ svayam ||

trimukhaḥ ṣaḍbhujaḥ śānto ratnācalaguhāśrayaḥ |
raktapadmasthite [kh: site] candre lalitākṣepasaṃsthitaḥ ||

sitapītamahānīlamūlavāmetarānanaḥ [For this caraṇa k: has sitapītamahānīsa 
vya vāmetasenanaḥ (?)] |
vyāghracarmāmbarastryakṣaḥ sarvābharaṇasundaraḥ ||

varadākṣaśarān savye vāme satkāminīkucam |
sitapadmaṃ ca kodaṇḍaṃ bibhrāṇaṃ karapallavaiḥ ||

kapālaṃ vāmato nyasya [k, b: vinyasya] nānāpuṣpaiḥ prapūritam |
viṣabhṛdveṣṭitaṃ savye triśūlaṃ ca tathāmbuje ||

amitābhamaṇisaccūḍaḥ kapālakṛtaśekharaḥ [k: kaupāla] |
śaśikhaṇḍayutottuṅgajaṭāmakuṭasañcayaḥ ||

vidhūtakalpanājālaḥ [k: drops vidhūtakalpanājālaḥ] 
sphuradbuddhaugharaśmikaḥ [k, b: sphuranbuddhau] |
itthaṃ hālāhalo bhūtvā svahṛtkaṇṭhaśiraḥsu ca ||

p. 74)

huṁ āḥ oṃ bhāvayeccandre nīlāruṇasitān kramāt |
hṛtka.ṭhayostathā madhye dhyeyo hrīḥ candrasaṃsthitaḥ ||

tataḥ sthiracalān [k, b: has sarva after sthiracalān] bhāvān 
bhāvayaṃstāṃstadākṛtīn |
sarvāsatkalpanirmukto japenmantraṃ samāhitaḥ ||

oṃ hālāhala hrīḥ |

antarjalpamatispaṣṭaṃ na drutaṃ na vilambitam |
yathāsukhaṃ japaṃ kṛtvā visṛṣṭau pratipūjya ca |
saṃstutya ca dṛḍhīkṛtya samuccārya śatākṣaram ||

tatredaṃ [tatredaṃ śatākṣaraṃ is dropped in k, kh, ḍh, g] 
śatākṣaram - oṃ vajrasattva samayamanupālaya vajrasattvatvenopatiṣṭha dṛḍho me 
bhava sutoṣyo me bhava supoṣyo me bhava anurakto me bhava sarvasiddhiṃ me prayaccha 
sarvakarmasu ca me cittaṃ śreyaḥ kuru huṁ hahahaha hoḥ bhagavan sarvatathāgatavajra mā 
me muñca vajrībhava mahāsamayasattva āḥ |

oṃ [ṭhe visarjana formula oṃ kṛto vaḥ etc. is not found in k] kṛto vaḥ 
sarvasattvārthaḥ siddhirdattā yathānugā |
gacchabdhaṃ buddhaviṣayaṃ punarāgamanāya ca ||

anena sañcodya enaṃ mantramuccārayet | oṃ akāro mukhaṃ 
sarvadharmāṇāmādyamanutpannatvāt [k, g: mādyanut] muriti |

dhyānāt samutthito yogī dānaṃ dadyāt svaśaktitaḥ [k; saśaṅkitaḥ, ḍh: 
saśaktitaḥ] |

p. 75)

rūpādikāmān bhuñjāno nirastāśeṣakalpanaḥ |
prāpnotyanuttarāṃ [k: omits from prāpnotyanuttarāṃ viśvapadmacandre 
including the colophon and part of the next sādhana] bodhimacirāt sādhakottamaḥ 
||

likhitvā sādhanaṃ prāptaṃ yanmayā śubhamuttamam [ḍh, g: sukhamu] 
|
tena kṛtsnaṃ jagad bhūyādacireṇa hālāhalaḥ ||

|| hālāhalasādhanaṃ samāptamiti [ḍh, g: hālāhalasādhanaṃ only] ||

30.

pūrvoktavidhānena viśvapadmacandre raktahīḥkārapariṇataṃ 
padmanarteśvaramātmānaṃ bhāvayet sattvaparyaṅkaniṣaṇaṃ dvibhujaikamukhaṃ 
raktaṃ sakalālaṅkāradharaṃ amitābhamukuṭaṃ vāmapārśve 
pāṇḍaravāsinīsamāśliṣṭaṃ āliṅganābhinayasthitavāmabhujena raktapadmadharaṃ 
nartanābhinayena sūcīmudrayā vikāśayadaparadakṣiṇakaram | tataḥ oṃ 
kāyavākcittavajrasvabhāvātmako'haṃ iti mantramuccārayet | tadanvaṣṭasu dikṣu 
aṣṭadevīṃ [k: sudevīṃ, g: vṛdevīṃ] cintayet | 
tatrāṣṭadalaraktapadmapūrvapatre [k: padmapatre, ḍh: padmapūrvayantre] 
vilokinī śuklā raktapadmadharā dakṣiṇapatre [ḍh: yantre] tārā haritā 
palāśapadmadharā paścimadale bhūriṇī cakranīlotpaladharā uttaradale bhṛkuṭī śuklā 
pītapadmadharā pūrvakoṇadale padmavāsinī pītā māñjiṣṭhapadmadharā

p. 76)

dakṣiṇakoṇadale vajrapadmeśvarī ākāśavarṇā sitapadmadharā paścimakṇadale 
viśvapadmā śuklā kṛṣṇapadmadharā uttarakoṇadale viśvavajrā viśvavarṇā 
viśvapadmadharā | sarvā etāḥ sattvaparyaṅkinyo dvibhujaikamukhāḥ saumyāḥ | 
karṇikāyāṃ tu bhagavāneva | tato mantraṃ japet | mantraḥ oṃ hrīḥ padmanarteśvara huṁ |

|| padmanarteśvaralokanāthasādhanam ||

31.

namaḥ [ṭhis sādhana is only to be found in ḍh, k] padmanarteśvarāya |

tatra viśvapadmopari candre raktahrīḥkārapariṇataṃ padmanarteśvaraṃ 
raktavarṇamekamukhaṃ jaṭāmukuṭinaṃ trinetraṃ aṣṭabhujaṃ sarvālaṅkārabhūṣitaṃ 
sarpayajñopavītamarddha paryaṅkena tāṇḍavaṃ prathamabhujadvayena nṛtyābhinayaṃ 
dvitīyadakṣiṇabhujena hṛdi vikāśayantaṃ sūcīmudrāṃ vāmabhujena raktapadmaṃ 
śirasi dhṛtaṃ tṛtīyabhujadvayena vajravaddanḍatriśūladharaṃ [ḍ, dh: has 
vajravidaṇḍi] caturthabhujadvayena akṣasūtrakuṇḍikādharaṃ aṣṭadevīparivṛtaṃ 
evaṃbhūtaṃ padmanarteśvaralokanāthaṃ bhāvayet |

|| padmanarteśvarasādhanam ||

p. 77)

32.

pūrvoktavidhānena [k: pūrkaika] śūnyatābhāvanānantaraṃ 
viśvapadmasthacandropari sitahrīḥkāraṃ vibhāvya tatpariṇāmena 
padmanartteśvarāmnāyenāryāvalokiteśvarabhaṭṭārakamātmānaṃ vibhāvayet [k: 
bhāva] | ekamukhaṃ aṣṭādaśabhujamarddhaparyaṅkinaṃ 
amitābhajaṭājūṭamaṇḍalaṃ sarvakarairviśvapadmadhāriṇam yoginīvṛndaparivṛtaṃ 
dakṣiṇavāmapārśvasthitatārāsudhanabhṛkuṭīhayagrīvaṃ [k: dakṣiṇapārśva] 
divyālaṅkāravastrabhūṣaṇaṃ | tato mantraṃ japet | oṃ hrīḥ padmanarteśvara huṁ | 
vyādhyādyupaśame mahānuśaṃsaḥ |

|| iti padmanarteśvarasādhanam ||

33.

pūrvoktavidhānena śūnyatābhāvanānantaraṃ sitahrīḥkāraniṣpannaṃ 
harihariharivāhanodbhavaṃ [k: harihara] bhagavantamāryāvalokiteśvaraṃ 
sarvāṅgaśuklaṃ jaṭāmakuṭinaṃ śāntaveśaṃ dakṣiṇakareṇa bhagavantaṃ tathāgataṃ 
sākṣiṇaṃ kurvantaṃ dvitīyena akṣamālādhāriṇaṃ [k: varadadhāriṇaṃ] 
tṛtīyena duḥkuhakaṃ lokamupadeśayantaṃ [k: mupadarśayantaṃ] vāmena 
daṇḍadharaṃ dvitīyena kṛṣṇājinadharaṃ [k: jinina] tṛtīyena 
kamaṇḍaludharaṃ siṃhagaruḍaviṣṇuskandhasaṃsthitamātmānaṃ [g: 
skandhaśata] dhyātvā oṃ hrīḥ huṁ iti mantraṃ japet |

|| harihariharivāhanodbhavasādhanam ||

p. 78)

34.

ādau tāvanmantrī sukhāsanāsīno jagadabhyuddharaṇāśayaḥ 
pāpadeśanādikasaptavidhānuttarapūjāṃ kṛtvā yaṁkāraniṣpannaṃ [k, ḍh: 
paṅkāra] vāyumaṇḍalaṃ dhanvākāraṃ nīlapatākāṅkitaṃ tasyopari rephodbhavaṃ 
raktavarṇamagnimaṇḍalaṃ trikoṇaṃ raktarekhāṅkitaṃ tadupari vaṁkārapariniṣpannaṃ 
[k, ḍh: caṅkāra] vāruṇamaṇḍalaṃ [kh: varuṇa] vartulaṃ 
śuklavarṇaṃ śuklapatākāṅkitaṃ [kh, ḍh: śuklaghaṭamadhyāṅkitaṃ] 
tadupari laṁkārasambhavaṃ māhendramaṇḍalaṃ caturasraṃ pītavarṇaṃ 
pītatrisūcikavajracatuṣkoṇaśobhitaṃ tadupari suṁkāraniṣpannaṃ saptaratnamayaṃ 
aṣṭāṅgaṃ sumeruṃ tasyopari paṁkārapariṇataṃ viśvadalakamalaṃ tasyopari ātmānaṃ 
[k: mātmānaṃ] vicintya oṃ hrīḥ huṁ krameṇa śirasi kaṇṭhe hṛdi 
vinyasyātmānaṃ anādikālasaṃcitakarmāvaraṇavigataṃ [k: kāsa ; k: dviyataṃ] 
harihariharivāhanodbhavaṃ lokeśvaramūrti suvarṇaratnaraśmijvālākulaṃ kumārarūpaṃ 
vicintayet mantrī | paścāt svahṛdi puṇyajñānaviśuddhyā candramaṇḍalaṃ 
sūryamaṇḍalaṃ ca tatra hrīḥkāravinirgataraśmijvālābhirjñānasattvamākṛṣya 
vakṣyamāṇarūpaṃ saṃpūjya pūrvoktapūjābhiḥ jñānasattvasamayasattvayorekīkaraṇāt 
bhagavantaṃ harihariharivāhanodbhavaṃ bhagavallokeśvaraṃ [kh, ḍh: omits 
bhagaval] sthiracittenātmānaṃ vibhāvayet ṣaḍbhujaṃ śuklavarṇaṃ jaṭāmukuṭinaṃ 
śāntaveśaṃ dakṣiṇakareṇa sambuddhaṃ sākṣiṇaṃ kurvantaṃ 
dvitīyenākṣamālādharaṃ tṛtīyena durgatisthitalokaṃ śubhamupadeśayantaṃ 
[śubhaṃ omitted in k] 

p. 79)

vāmena daṇḍadharaṃ dvitīyena kṛṣṇājinadharaṃ tṛtīyena kamaṇḍaludharaṃ 
bhagavantaṃ cintayet | tatra viśuddhiḥ | vāyvagnimaṇḍalābhyāṃ hariḥ vāruṇamaṇḍalena 
garuḍaḥ māhendramaṇḍalasumerubhyāṃ [ḍh: sukhahabhyāṃ] haririti viśuddhiḥ 
satataṃ bhāvayan yogī sarvasattvārthakaraṇasamartho [k: ṇaṃ artho] bhavati 
sarvajanapriyo bhavati mahāprājño bhavati nīrogo nirupadravaśca dhanāḍhyo bhavati 
vaśaviṣakarmaṇi [k: śava] mahāsamartho bhavati | japamantraṃ gurūpadeśato 
jñeyam [k: kṣayaṃ] |

|| harihariharivāhanodbhavalokeśvarasādhanam ||

35.

prathamaṃ tāvat mano'nukūle sthāne yogipaṭaṃ [k: sthāne paṭhaṃ, kh: sthāne 
paṭaṃ] prakāśya tasyāgrato māhendramaṇḍalamadhye kuṅkumena [k: 
kuṅkume] candramaṇḍalaṃ kārayet oṃ vajrarekhe huṁ iti mantreṇa | tadanu jaḥ huṁ 
vaṁ padmasthahoriti mantreṇāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā maṇḍalamadhye 
trailokyavaśaṅkaralokeśvarabhaṭṭārakaṃ pūjayedanena mantreṇa oṃ 
trailokyavaśaṅkarāya vajrapuṣpe huṁ pratīccha [k: pratiṣṭha] svāhā | tataḥ oṃ 
munivajre [kh: maṇivajre] muriti visarjya svahṛdi rakta-āṁkāraṃ vicintya 

p. 80)

tatpariṇāmena sūryaṃ tadupari raktahrīḥkāraṃ tadraśmisamākṛṣṭāmitābhatathāgataṃ 
saṃpūjya pāpadeśanādikaṃ kṛtvā trailokyaṃ ca [k: trailokyavaśaṃ] vaśaṃ 
vidhāya raśmīn svabīje praveśya tatpariṇāmena jhaṭiti lokeśvaraṃ 
sarvāṅgamahārāgaraktamekamukhaṃ dvibhujaṃ trinetraṃ jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍitaṃ 
vajrāṅkitapāśāṅkuśahastaṃ raktapadme vajraparyaṅkaniṣaṇaṃ 
divyābharaṇavastravibhūṣitamātmānaṃ [k, kh: vastrabhū] vicintya 
tribhuvanameṣadvayasthapāśavajroparisthitanānānirmāṇadhāribuddhabodhisattva##-
hrīḥ huṁ yāṁ [k, ḍh: yoṁ, ḍh, a; āḥ, g: yīṃ] iti jāpya 
tryakṣaramantraṃ [k: akṣara] dhyātvā oṃ 
śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'haṃ oṃ vajrātmakāḥ sarvadharmā vajrātmako'haṃ 
iti vajrātmakamantradvayaṃ paṭhet [k: japet] | evamabhyasyato 
dhunanakampanādiguṇa [k, g: kampanādiṣu notpadyate] utpadyate | tadanu oṃ 
hrīḥ [k, g, ḍh, ḍh a: hrīṃ] aḥ hāṁ hārīti yakṣeśvarī svāhā iti 
piṇḍaṃ dadyāt | oṃ āṁ kroṁ hrīṁ sakalagaṇavīravīreśvarīṇāṃ [g: 
vīreśvarāṇāṃ] parikarai a a avatara avatara avatarantu daśadiglokapālā idaṃ baliṃ 
gṛhṇa gṛhṇa huṁ svāhā iti baliṃ dattvā devatāyogena viharediti | 

|| sarahapādakṛtaṃ oḍḍiyānakrameṇa [k, ḍh, g, ḍh, a: 
oḍyāyana] trailokyavaśaṅkaralokeśvarasādhanaṃ samāptam ||

p. 81)

namo [ṭhis namaskāra is absent in all ṃṣṣ except k, b] lokanāthāya |

vajrarekhe huṁ | jaḥ huṁ vaṁ padmasthahoḥ anena mantreṇāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā 
maṇḍalamadhye trailokyavaśaṅkaralokeśvarabhaṭṭārakaṃ pūjayet anena mantreṇa oṃ 
trailokyavaśaṅkarāya vajrapuṣpe huṁ pratīccha svāhā | oṃ munivajre muḥ [k: 
muniḥ] visarjanamantro'yam [k, b: mantraḥ] | paścāt svahṛdi 
āṁkāraṃ cintayet | taṃ pariṇamya sūryamaṇḍalaṃ niṣpadyate | tasyopari śaktibījaṃ 
bālāruṇasamaprabhaṃ draṣṭavyam | etasya raśmibhiramitābhaṃ [k, b: adds 
tathāgataṃ after amitābhaṃ] pūjayitvā pāpadeśanādikaṃ kṛtvā punaḥ śarīre 
praviśantaṃ etat sarvaṃ pariṇamya jhaṭiti padmoparisthaṃ vajraparyaṅkinaṃ [k, 
kh, g, ḍh: paryaṅkaṃ] vikasitatrinetraṃ jaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇaṃ raktavarṇaṃ

[k: pāśavaddhe tadbhe tasyo, ḍh, g: pāśavaddhe tasyo] sthitā 
nānānirmāṇadhāriṇyo vajrayoginyo'bhiṣekaṃ prayacchanti tathā bodhisattvā 
nānābharaṇayuktāḥ [k, g: saṃyuktā, ḍh: muktā] pūjāṃ kurvanti 
vidyādharahariharahiraṇyagarbhanānāyoginīgaṇā

p. 82)

nṛtyavādyādikaṃkurvanti nānābhūtagaṇādinānānaraśiraḥkapālamālāvṛtaṃ tathā

draṣṭavyaḥ | īdṛśamātmānaṃ vicintayet | paścānnābherupari sūryamaṇḍalaṃ 
raśmimayaṃ vicintya tasyopari vāme śaktibījaṃ hṛdi krodhabījaṃ 
āḥkārasvarabhūṣitaṃ iti bhāvyamantraḥ [k, b: reads iti bhaṭṭārakasya 
hṛdayamantrah] | etat sarvaṃ dhyātvā vajrātmakamantroccāraṇaṃ kṛtvā

kampanāveśaṃ] kṛtvā advaitajñānamutpadyate | sa yogī dviradavat garjati 
[garjati present only in k, b] madirāmatta iva pañcavarṇaṃ viharati | yadi

deśanādvaitajñānādikaṃ] na tiṣṭhati | evaṃ pratītyaiva [ḍh: pratītya] 
pañcavarṇaṃ vihartavyam [k, ḍh, g: vihartavyaṃ] tena siddhiḥ syāt | 
siddhe sati paradehe dhunanakampanāveśanaṃ kṛtvā rāgadveṣādikaṃ niṣedhayati | 
yathā siddharasena viddhaṃ tāmraṃ kṛṣṇatāṃ tyajati tathā śarīramevādvaitarasena 
viddhaṃ rāgadveṣādikaṃ tyajati | hrīḥ [k, kh, g: huṁ huṁ yāṁ, k, 
kh: hrīṁḥ huṁ aṁ, ḍh: hrīṃ huṁ āḥ] huṁ yāṁ japyamantraḥ | oṃ hrīḥ 
[k, g, ḍh: oṃ hrīṁ aḥ, k, b: oṃ aḥ, kh: oṃ hrīḥ aḥ] aḥ 
hāṁ hārīti yakṣeśvarī svāhā hārītimantraḥ | oṃ āṁ kroṁ 

p. 83)

namo lokanāthāya |

paścāt punaridamārabheta - 

grāhyagrāhakahānito jagadidaṃ svapnendrajālopamaṃ śuddhaṃ ca 
prakṛtiprabhāsvaratayā vyomopamāmāśritam |
ātmānaṃ ca manovilāsakalitaṃ niścitya saṃkṣepato muḥkāraṃ 
punaraṃśujālajaṭilaṃ tatsambhavaṃ bhāvayet ||

tataḥ parāvṛttamakhaṇḍamaṇḍalaṃ [k: makhaṇḍalaṃ] himārcciṣaḥ 
kuṅkumapaṅkapiñjaram |
svarojjvalanmālikayā samākulaṃ vicintayed dīdhitidīptadiṅmukham ||

tataḥ punarmuḥkṛtibījasambhavaṃ vibhāvayed rāgamarīcimaṇḍalam |
kakhādivarṇairakhilaṃ [k, ḍh, g, k, b: rakhilai] 
samāvṛtaṃ [k, b: samāsṛtaṃ] 
tadūrdhvamuddīdhitikuṅkumāruṇam ||

p. 91)

tasmiṃśca muḥkāramudīkṣya mantrī tatsambhavaṃ bhāsvarapatraramyam [k: 
puṭa] |
indīvaraṃ visphuradaṃśujālaṃ [k: visphuraṇa] 
vicintayenmuḥkṛtibījamadhyam ||

tatsambhūtamudaṃśubuddhavisarairāpūrayantaṃ jagat candrasthaṃ 
bhramarāndhabhāsvaratarairudbhāsvaraṃ [k, g: bhāsura ; dbhāsura] 
cīrakaiḥ |
dhunvantaṃ nibiḍāndhakārapaṭalaṃ [k: niviḍārtha] raktāṃśujālairalaṃ 
sarvākāravarapradānanipuṇaṃ śrīmadgirāmīśvaram [k, ḍh, b: 
śrīmangi0] ||

lālityaśṛṅgārarasābhirāmaṃ [k: rālibhya0, ḍh: rālitya0] 
vyājṛmbhamāṇāmburuhāsyalakṣmīm [k: vyākṣambha, ḍh: vyajyanta, 
g: vyajjambha] |
vīraṃ [kh: bījaṃ] kumārābharaṇaṃ dadhānaṃ dhyāyāt padaṃ tasya 
samīhamānaḥ ||

evaṃ vicintya vāgīśaṃ muḥkāraṃ bhāvayed hṛdi |
jvalantaṃ raktavarṇābhaṃ nirgacchaddīptimaṇḍalam ||

tasmāt sañjātamaṣṭāracakraṃ hārddatamopaham |
pūrayed raśmibhirviśvaṃ [k: śmibhiśca] paśyet jñānamayaṃ prabhum 
||

nirmitaṃ bālatigmārccīrociṣāṃ [k: timmārcāvāviṣāṃ, ḍh: 
timmārcārociṣāṃ, k, b: timmārcīrorciṣāṃ] sañcayairiva |
ākīrṇamakṣarai [kh: ākīrṇya cākṣa] raktai raktapuṣpotkarairiva ||

p. 92)

tatraivaṃ [k, g: tenaiva] tāvadakṣarāṇi cintayet cakrasyāreṣu 
pūrvadakṣiṇapaścimottareṣu yathākramaṃ arapacanān [k, g: cārapattāna, 
k, b: arapacan, ḍh: arapacān] nābhau nakāraṃ āgneyanaiṛtyavāyavya##-
kakārādīni vyañjanāni iti niścalaṃ niścitya paścāccakrasya varṇasaṃhateśca 
raśmisamūhaṃn hārddaṃ tamomaṇḍalamapanayantaṃ [k, b: tamoha ; k, g: 
vayakaṃ] svaśarīraromakūpavivarebhyo niḥsṛtya 
daśadiglokadhātuvyavasthitatathāgatahṛdayajñānāmṛtaiḥ [tathāgata dropped in 
k, b] saha [k: sahasra sarasī] samarasībhūya tebhyo nirgatya taireva 
romavivaraiḥ [k: taineṣvavāma] praviśya svaśarīramavabhāsayantaṃ ciraṃ 
dhyāyāt | sthiracittasya paścāt sphuraṇasaṃharaṇanyāyena tāvad bhāvayet yāvat 
pratyakṣamābhāti |

mañjuśriyo dvaitadaśāṃ [k, g: daśyandadhāne, ḍh: 
daśāndadhāne] dadhānaiḥ sahaiva [k: sahaṃ vavarṇeḥ] varṇaiḥ 
parirocamānaiḥ [k, ḍh: variroca] |
cakre sphuṭābhatvamupāgate'smin aharnniśaṃ dhyānakṛtābhiyogāt || 

adṛṣṭapūrveṣvāpi vāṅmayeṣu kalākalāpeṣvākhileṣu tasya |
matiryatheṣṭaṃ [k, b: yati] maṇidīpadīptā [k, g: dīpta] 
pravarttate hārddatamo nudantī ||

p. 93)

idaṃ hi cakraṃ hitamīhamānaiḥ kutūhalenāpi ca pakṣamekam |

dbhavatsu] ||

manasi] dīpyatāṃ [k: dīvyatāṃ] tena cakram |
navataraṇirivāstamohasāndrāndhakāraṃ [k, ḍh, g: mivāsyāṃ, 
k, b: mivāsyaṃ, kh: virāsyan] vrajatu [k, g: vrajatu vajrana] 
ca janateyaṃ vāgvibhoḥ śrīvilāsam ||

sthiracakramidaṃ śrīmanmañjuvajrasya tāyinaḥ |
taṃ natvā bālabodhāya likhyate viśadaiḥ padaiḥ ||

viśvaṃ cittamātraṃ [k: mātrī] 

p. 94)

grāhyagrāhakavivarjitaṃ [k, ḍh, g: varjitaṃ] svapnavat 
pratibimbavadātmānaṃ niścitya kuṅkumāruṇaṃ muḥkārajātaṃ [k, 
kāraṃ, kh, ḍh: kārajaṃ, g: kāraja0] sphuradakārādisvararaśmiraktaṃ 
[k: raśmicanraṃ, ḍh: raśmicakraṃ] cakraṃ paśyet | tasyopari muḥkārajaṃ 
bhāsvatkakārādiprabhāraktaṃ śaśāṅkamaṇḍalaṃ bhāvayet | tasyopari muḥkārajaṃ 
[k: mukāraṃ, g: aḥkārajaṃ] sphuradamalamindīvaraṃ 
muḥkārādhiṣṭhitakiñjalkaṃ vicintya tatpariṇataṃ [k, b: tatsarvaṃ]

[kh: mālikāliko0] | tasya ca nābhau akāraṃ pūrvāre rephaṃ dakṣiṇāre pakāraṃ 
paścimāre cakāraṃ uttarāre nakāraṃ āgneyādikoṇeṣu ca niṣprapañcānakṣarān [k: 
kurān] kuṅkumābhān vicintya taccakraraśmisamūhaṃ antaḥśarīramavabhāsya 
[k, g: mantraḥśarīraṃ] pratiromakūpavivarairnniḥsṛtya

|

tena bhūyāt jagat sarvaṃ mañjuvajrasamaprabham ||

|| muktena sthiracakrasādhanam ||

46.

namaḥ śrīvādirāḍmañjaghoṣāya 

prathamaṃ tāvat mukhaprakṣālanādikaṃ kṛtvā mṛdusukhāsanopaviṣṭaḥ [k: 
sukhamukhā] svahṛdi paṁkārajapadmasyopari akāreṇa candramaṇḍalaṃ tasyopari 
maṁkāraraśminākṛṣyākaniṣṭhabhuvanavartti anādisaṃsiddhamañjuśrīkumāraṃ 
purato vibhāvya tato bāhyapūjāṃ puṣpadhūpādikāṃ kṛtvā abhivandya ca paścāt 
pāpadeśanāṃ puṇyānumodanāṃ puṇyapariṇāmanāṃ triśaraṇagamanaṃ 
bodhicittotpādaṃ adhyeṣaṇāṃ ātmabhāvaniryātanāṃ kṛtvā caturbrahmavihārān 
bhāvayet | punaḥ sakalavastutattvasārasaṃgrāhakabhūtāṃ śūnyatāṃ [śūnyatāṃ is 
dropped in k, g] vicintayet [ḍh: adds kuṅkumābhāsaṃ ciraṃ vicintayet after 
vicintayet] oṃ śūnyatājñānavajrasvabhāvātmako'hamiti | punaḥ 
praṇidhānamanusmṛtya pratibhāsamātraṃ svakāyaṃ vyavalokya svahṛdi 
padmacandrasyopari maṁkārabījaraśminā sarvatathāgatapraveśenātmānaṃ 
mañjuśrīrūpaṃ vibhāvayet | pītavarṇaṃ vyākhyānamudrādharaṃ ratnābharaṇaṃ 
ratnamukuṭinaṃ vāmenotpalaṃ siṃhāsanasthaṃ akṣobhyākrāntamaulinaṃ candrāsanaṃ 
[k: cakrāsanaṃ] candraprabhaṃ bhāvayedātmānam | tato dakṣiṇapārśve 
suṁkārabījasambhavaṃ sudhanakumāraṃ nānāratnābharaṇojjvalaṃ ratnamukuṭinaṃ 
[k: saṃratna0] sarvadharmaikapustakakakṣānikṣiptaṃ saṃpuṭāñjalipūrvakaṃ 
tiṣṭhet | vāmapārśve yamāriḥ kṛṣṇavarṇahuṁkārabījodbhavaḥ vikṛtānanaḥ 
mudgarahastaḥ piṅgorddhvakeśaḥ 

p. 96)

nāgābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ | tato dakṣiṇottarapārśve candraprabhasūryaprabhau 
pūrvādidigbhāge vairocanaratnasambhavāmitābhāmoghasiddhayaḥ āgneyādikoṇeṣu

|| mañjuśrīsādhanam ||

47.

satsaukhyaṃ paramaṃ samastajagatāmicchoranicchoḥ [k: icchoḥ sukhaṃ]

saṃlikhyate mandasvalpadhiyā mayā bhagavato mañjuśriyaḥ sādhanam ||

sakalākuśalakriyāpi |
tenaiva śuddhamanasā guruto niśamya dhyānādikaṃ bhagavataḥ satataṃ vidheyam 
||

ādau manobhirucite nicite sugandhipuṣpādibhirbahuvidhairvijanapradeśe [k, 
g: nicite] |

p. 97)

cauradhvanipramukhakaṇṭakavarjite ca nirvartya [k: nirvṛtya, g:

yatnataścañcalaṃ cittamekāgrīkṛtya nirbharam |
parārthamāśaye kṛtvā bhāvayet tadanantaram ||

sattveṣvekatanujatoṣasamatāsaṃlakṣitāṃ [k: sattvevaka] mitratāṃ

abhyuddidhārṣā] dayām |
cittaprītiviśeṣalakṣaṇavatīṃ [k: vibhu] 
sammodikāmantato'sadvyāsaṅganivṛttarūpasahitāṃ dhyāyādupekṣāṃ budhaḥ ||

tadanu manasi kṛtvā yāvadicchāprakīrṇaṃ vimalarucihimārcirmaṇḍalaṃ 
śuklavarṇam |
tadupari sukumāraṃ nirmalasvarṇavarṇaṃ [k, g: nirbhara svarnaṃ] 
kamalamamalapatraṃ [k, g: kamalapatraṃ] bhāvayedaṣṭapatram ||

candrāmbhoruhayorudaṃśuvisaravyālolayoruttamāmūrdhvaṃ 
varddhanaśīlaraśmikuharaṃ svarṇākṛtiṃ maṁkṛtim |
pūrvābhyāṃ pihitaṃ hitāya jagatāṃ mantrī ciraṃ bhāvayet |
atyantottamamañjuvajrapadavīmicchan [k: atyantokama0] manuṣyottamaḥ ||

raśmibhiḥ |

p. 98)

paścād dikṣu daśasvavasthitatanūnanīya tai raśmibhiḥ sarvāneva gurūṃstathaiva 
sugatān śrībodhisattvānapi |
kṛtvā tān purato manorathamayaṃ niṣpādya pūjāvidhiṃ 
nānāpuṣpasugandhidhūpavividhākāreṇa [k: sugandhibhirvi0] saṃpūjayet ||

pāpāderapi deśanādipuratasteṣāṃ vidadhyāt tataḥ 
tānevāṃśubhirekatāmupagatāna [k: sthāne] romāvalīnāṃ [k, g: 
līrāñcinaiḥ] bilaiḥ |
puñjākāratayā praviśya hṛdaye dṛṣṭvā tatastadbhavaṃ dhyāyan 
mohamahādripāṭanapaṭuṃ [k: puṭaṃ] śrīmañjuvajraṃ vibhum ||

svacchaṃ ṣoḍaśavatsarākṛtidharaṃ śārddūlapṛṣṭhasthitaṃ 
vyākhyāvyākulapāṇipadmayugalaṃ vāmārddhaparyaṅkinam |
dīrghaṃ nāpi na cāpi kharvamasamaṃ saundaryarāśyāśrayaṃ 
ratnasvarṇamaṇiprakāravividhālaṅkāramālākulam ||

nāsatyaṃ nāpyasatyaṃ mukuratalamiva spaṣṭabimbāyamānaṃ dhyātvā vīraṃ 
[k, g: ciraṃ] kumāraṃ sakalajanaparitrāṇadaṃ [k, g: 
paritrāṇaṃ] vādinātham [k, g: devādināthaṃ] |
paścādātmānameva vyapagatakaluṣaṃ mañjuvajrasya mūrttyā dhyāyed dīrghaṃ 
na yāvad bhavati bhagavatā tulyadeho manīṣī ||

tāntaṃ rāntaṃ [k, ḍh: nāntaṃ] tathā sāntaṃ vyañjanakramasaṃhitam 
|
dhyānāt khinno japenmantraṃ okāraṃ [k: sakāraṃ, g: sekāraṃ, ḍh: 
maikāraṃ] sānunāsikam ||

aparamiha viśeṣaṃ sadguroreva vācā [g: vāco] vimalamamalabuddheḥ 
pragrahīṣyanti santaḥ |

p. 99)

pravaragurukṛtājño dhyānamantrakriyāyāḥ nahi sakalaviśeṣakhyāpane'haṃ 
samarthaḥ ||

[k: hinasti] ||

śrīmanmañjukumārasādhanamidaṃ samyag gurorājñayā kṛtvā mandadhiyā 
mayā yadamitaṃ [k, g: padamitaṃ] puṇyaṃ samāsāditam |
tenāyāsamapāsya satvaramayaṃ śrīmañjuvajropamaḥ 
śrīcintāmaṇikalpavṛkṣasadṛśo'pyāstāṃ samasto janaḥ ||

|| śrīmadvādirāṭsādhanaṃn samāptam ||

|| kṛtiriyaṃ paṇḍitaśrīcintāmaṇidattasyeti ||

namo mañjunāthāya |

aṃ-candramaṇḍalasyorddhvaṃ hrīṁkāraṃ kuṅkumāruṇam ||

p. 100)

dṛṣṭvā vadanaśaucādiṃ [ḍh: dikaṃ, k, g: di] kṛtvā 
dhyānālaye śucau |
sukhāsanaṃ ca saṃviśya mantraṃ buddhaguṇākaram ||

maitrīṃ satputrasamprītisahasraguṇitāṃ jane |
duḥkhanetordduḥkhācca kṛpāmuddharttukāmanām [k: murddhanta, ḍh: 
muddhartya] ||

nityaṃ sadguṇasaṃyojanecchāṃ pramuditāṃ tathā |
nijakāryamanālocyopekṣāmanyārthakāritām ||

dhyātvā mantrī [mantrī dropped in k, g] sphuradśmivyūhenānīya 
[k: reads phaṇānāṃ yatān jātānu] tān jinān |
tairnabhaḥ pūritaṃ dṛṣṭvā kṛtvā pūjāṃ manomayīm ||

abhyaṅgodvarttanasnānanānālaṅkārakāriṇaḥ [k: nabhānālakṣara] |
sphuratpūjaughasanmeghān viśvak [k: viśvaṃ saṃsthārya devatīṃ, g: 
viśvakāsphārya devatīṃ, ḍh: viśvak saṃsphūrya devatīṃ] samsphārya 
devatām ||

praṇamya nirmitaiḥ kāyairasaṃkhyeyaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
rāgavidveṣasammohaistrividhaṃ kāyamānasam ||

saṃvegād deśayet [k: śayātyāya] pāpaṃ kṛtakāritamoditam |
āyatyāṃ saṃvaraṃ sattvaiḥ sārddhaṃn kuryāt punaḥ punaḥ ||

saṃbuddhamañjughoṣādyairddānādijanitaṃ śubham |
saṃmodyādhyeṣya saṃyācya ciraṃ saddharmadeśanām ||

triratnaśaraṇaṃ gatvā bodhicittaṃ sanmārgakam [k: sanmārjakaṃ] |
āśritya oṃ śūnyatājñānavajrātmako'ham [k, g: 
vajrasvabhāvātmako] ||

p. 101)

tato viśvapadmasthaṃ maṁkārendūrdhvasaṃsthitam |
sphuratpiṇḍākṣaraṃ dṛṣṭvā kṛtvā mañjvaraṃ jagat ||

praviśyākṛṣya sanmantrī vādirājaṃ vibhāvayet |
kāśmīrāruṇasatkāyamāyānirjitamanmatham ||

siṃhasiṃhāsanākrāntaṃ nirbhrāntaṃ lalitāsanam |
vāmaprakoṣṭhapūrveṣu [k: niṣṭharthaṃ, ḍh: nistabdhaṃ] 
nīlendīvaradhāriṇam ||

saṃbuddhaguṇasandohaṃ ratnālaṅkārasojjvalam |
niryadraśmisphuradbuddhasandohālokamālinam ||

dharmacakramahāmudrānibaddhakarapaṅkajam |
tadvadindīvare tādṛk viśvakṛnmantraraśminā [k, g: kṛt] ||

samānīya paraṃ nāthaṃ kūṭāgārādibhiḥ samam [k, g: svayaṃ] |
sadṛśatvaṃ samāpādya [k: sādya] saṃśrāvya samayaṃ tridhā |
samākṛṣya praveśyātha buddhaṃ samyak vaśaṃ nayet ||

oṃ vajrasattva samayadṛśyahoḥ samayastvaṃ samayo'haṃ samayastvamahaṃ jaḥ huṁ 
vaṁ hoḥ | oṃ kāyādhiṣṭhānaṃ āḥ vāgadhiṣṭhānaṃ huṁ cittādhiṣṭhānaṃ | 
utpalamudrayā piṇḍākṣareṇa jñānādhiṣṭhānaṃ ca kṛtvā

[k: kumbhāvu, ḍh: kumbho avu0] bhavet |

śubhaṃ tena janāḥ sarve mantrasambuddhabhāskarāḥ [ṭhen follows the same

namastārāyai [ṭhis sādhana is found only in kh. ṣee Fol. 79 k] |

p. 125, p. 223)

atha bhagavatyā hṛdayakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | oṃ asijihve śūlajihve vajrakāye grasa 
grasa jvala jvala mahājvāle mahāyogeśvari huṁ huṁ phaṭ phaṭ svāhā |

| trikālasnāyī madyamāṃsavasāpalāṇḍutailalavaṇavivarjitastrikālaṃ japet yāvad

kārayedanena vinyāsena |

prathamaṃ tāvanmantrī ātmānamīdṛśaṃ cintayet kumāryākāraṃ haritavarṇaṃ

nūpuramekhalāvalayakuṇḍalādīn sarpākārān cintayet | ekaikasmāt romavivarāt 
agnijvālāṃ samantānniṣkāśamānāṃ vicintayet | evamātmani kalpayet ahaṃ tāvat devatā

tu] ||
